Abstract: In this paper, we consider the problem of conditional anomaly detection that aims to identify data instances with an unusual response or a class label. We develop a new non-parametric approach for conditional anomaly detection based on the soft harmonic solution, with which we estimate the confidence of the label to detect anomalous mislabeling. We further regularize the solution to avoid the detection of isolated examples and examples on the boundary of the distribution support. We demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed method on several synthetic and UCI ML datasets in detecting unusual labels when compared to several baseline approaches. We also evaluate the performance of our method on a real-world electronic health record dataset where we seek to identify unusual patient-management decisions.

</details>


### [111] [Dynamical Priors as a Training Objective in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21464)
*Sukesh Subaharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DP-RL通过引入外部状态动力学的辅助损失，在不修改奖励、环境或策略架构的情况下，引导强化学习智能体的决策时序结构化，解决标准RL中出现的突兀信心转移、振荡或退化性静止等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习(RL)在优化奖励时对决策的时间演化约束很少，导致策略可能出现时间不一致的行为，如突兀的信心转移、振荡或退化性静止。

Method: 引入动力学先验强化学习(DP-RL)框架，通过外部状态动力学实现证据积累和磁滞效应，从中导出辅助损失来塑造学习过程中动作概率的时间演化。

Result: 在三个最小化环境中，动态度先验系统性地以任务依赖的方式改变决策轨迹，促进了时间结构化行为，这无法用通用平滑解释。

Conclusion: 仅通过训练目标即可控制RL智能体决策的时间几何结构。

Abstract: Standard reinforcement learning (RL) optimizes policies for reward but imposes few constraints on how decisions evolve over time. As a result, policies may achieve high performance while exhibiting temporally incoherent behavior such as abrupt confidence shifts, oscillations, or degenerate inactivity. We introduce Dynamical Prior Reinforcement Learning (DP-RL), a training framework that augments policy gradient learning with an auxiliary loss derived from external state dynamics that implement evidence accumulation and hysteresis. Without modifying the reward, environment, or policy architecture, this prior shapes the temporal evolution of action probabilities during learning. Across three minimal environments, we show that dynamical priors systematically alter decision trajectories in task-dependent ways, promoting temporally structured behavior that cannot be explained by generic smoothing. These results demonstrate that training objectives alone can control the temporal geometry of decision-making in RL agents.

</details>


### [112] [Drug Synergy Prediction via Residual Graph Isomorphism Networks and Attention Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21473)
*Jiyan Song,Wenyang Wang,Chengcheng Yan,Zhiquan Han,Feifei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In the treatment of complex diseases, treatment regimens using a single drug often yield limited efficacy and can lead to drug resistance. In contrast, combination drug therapies can significantly improve therapeutic outcomes through synergistic effects. However, experimentally validating all possible drug combinations is prohibitively expensive, underscoring the critical need for efficient computational prediction methods. Although existing approaches based on deep learning and graph neural networks (GNNs) have made considerable progress, challenges remain in reducing structural bias, improving generalization capability, and enhancing model interpretability. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a collaborative prediction graph neural network that integrates molecular structural features and cell-line genomic profiles with drug-drug interactions to enhance the prediction of synergistic effects. We introduce a novel model named the Residual Graph Isomorphism Network integrated with an Attention mechanism (ResGIN-Att). The model first extracts multi scale topological features of drug molecules using a residual graph isomorphism network, where residual connections help mitigate over-smoothing in deep layers. Subsequently, an adaptive Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) module fuses structural information from local to global scales. Finally, a cross-attention module is designed to explicitly model drug-drug interactions and identify key chemical substructures. Extensive experiments on five public benchmark datasets demonstrate that ResGIN-Att achieves competitive performance, comparing favorably against key baseline methods while exhibiting promising generalization capability and robustness.

</details>


### [113] [Generalizing Numerical Reasoning in Table Data through Operation Sketches and Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21495)
*Hanjun Cho,Gahyun Yoo,Hanseong Kim,Jay-Yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TaNOS通过表头匿名化、操作草图和程序优先的自监督预训练解决表格数值推理的跨域迁移问题，在FinQA上以10%数据超越SFT全量数据和GPT-5等闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家领域表格数值推理模型虽在本领域准确率高，但监督微调易产生表头-操作捷径依赖，对领域迁移鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出TaNOS持续预训练框架，包含：(1)表头匿名化减少词汇记忆；(2)操作草图提供结构线索；(3)程序优先的自监督预训练构建保证正确性的程序-问题对，从而解耦领域语义与数值操作结构。

Result: 在FinQA上，10%训练数据达到80.13%准确率，超越全量SFT基线(73.97%)及GPT-5/Gemini-2.5-Pro；领域迁移实验中，SFT跨域差距>10pp，TaNOS仅<2pp。

Conclusion: 结构化操作引导、表头无关表示和保证正确性的自监督可有效提升数值推理的跨领域鲁棒性。

Abstract: Numerical reasoning over expert-domain tables often exhibits high in-domain accuracy but limited robustness to domain shift. Models trained with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on specific datasets tend to rely on header-operation shortcuts rather than structural reasoning. We introduce TaNOS, a continual pre-training framework comprising three components: (i) header anonymization to reduce lexical memorization, (ii) operation sketches that provide minimal structural cues, and (iii) self-supervised pretraining that constructs correctness-guaranteed program-question pairs from given tables in a program-first manner. By decoupling domain semantics and numerical operation structure, TaNOS improves the transferability of numerical reasoning. Applied to an 8B instruction-tuned model, TaNOS achieves 80.13% execution accuracy on FinQA with only 10% train data, outperforming SFT baseline (73.97%) with full train data and proprietary models such as GPT-5, Gemini-2.5-Pro. Furthermore, in the domain-shift experiments, TaNOS displays nearly-negligible cross-domain gap (<2pp) when standard SFT shows over 10pp gap. These results suggest that structural guidance with operation sketches, header-agnostic representations, and correctness-guaranteed self-supervision can improve the robustness of numerical reasoning across diverse expert-domain tables.

</details>


### [114] [Large-Scale Data Parallelization of Product Quantization and Inverted Indexing Using Dask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21645)
*Ashley N. Abraham,Andrew Strelzoff,Haley R. Dozier,Althea C. Henslee,Mark A. Chappell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合产品量化（PQ）、倒排索引和 Dask 的大规模最近邻搜索近似方法，通过分而治之策略有效降低计算复杂度和内存开销，同时保持搜索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模最近邻搜索面临计算资源瓶颈，精确相似性搜索的计算开销过高，迫切需要一种能够在可接受精度范围内显著降低计算成本的方法。

Method: 采用分而治之的策略，利用产品量化（PQ）进行高效聚类，结合倒排索引优化检索效率，并使用 Dask 实现分布式计算，最后合并各分区结果。

Result: 在不牺牲准确性的前提下，将处理大规模数据的计算需求降低至中等规模数据的水平。

Conclusion: 通过 PQ、倒排索引和 Dask 的结合，实现了在无需额外硬件资源的情况下处理大规模数据，同时保持原有算法的准确性。

Abstract: Large-scale Nearest Neighbor (NN) search, though widely utilized in the similarity search field, remains challenged by the computational limitations inherent in processing large scale data. In an effort to decrease the computational expense needed, Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) search is often used in applications that do not require the exact similarity search, but instead can rely on an approximation. Product Quantization (PQ) is a memory-efficient ANN effective for clustering all sizes of datasets. Clustering large-scale, high dimensional data requires a heavy computational expense, in both memory-cost and execution time. This work focuses on a unique way to divide and conquer the large scale data in Python using PQ, Inverted Indexing and Dask, combining the results without compromising the accuracy and reducing computational requirements to the level required when using medium-scale data.

</details>


### [115] [Evaluating Post-hoc Explanations of the Transformer-based Genome Language Model DNABERT-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21690)
*Isabel Kurth,Paulo Yanez Sarmiento,Bernhard Y. Renard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将AttnLRP（一种基于注意力机制的层级相关性传播方法）适配到Transformer架构的基因组语言模型DNABERT-2，并提出从token和核苷酸级别转移解释的策略。实验表明，AttnLRP能为基因组语言模型生成与已知生物学模式相符的可靠解释，证明gLMs与CNNs一样能够提供有价值的生物学洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在基因组分析中表现优异，但模型的黑箱特性限制了对其预测结果的理解。CNN的解释方法已被证明有效，但更强大的Transformer-based基因组语言模型（gLMs）的可解释性尚不明确。

Method: 将AttnLRP从CNN扩展到Transformer架构的注意力机制；提出从token级别和核苷酸级别转移解释的策略；在多个基因组数据集上使用多种评估指标进行验证；将DNABERT-2的解释与CNN基线进行广泛比较。

Result: AttnLRP能够为gLMs生成与已知生物学模式相符的可靠解释；gLMs与CNNs一样可以帮助提取生物学洞察；验证了注意力机制可解释性方法在不同架构间的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 基因组语言模型具备可解释性，其解释结果与生物学先验知识相符；gLMs可作为生物学发现的有效工具；该工作为gLMs的可解释性研究奠定基础，并解决了跨架构相关性归因的可比性问题。

Abstract: Explaining deep neural network predictions on genome sequences enables biological insight and hypothesis generation-often of greater interest than predictive performance alone. While explanations of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have been shown to capture relevant patterns in genome sequences, it is unclear whether this transfers to more expressive Transformer-based genome language models (gLMs). To answer this question, we adapt AttnLRP, an extension of layer-wise relevance propagation to the attention mechanism, and apply it to the state-of-the-art gLM DNABERT-2. Thereby, we propose strategies to transfer explanations from token and nucleotide level. We evaluate the adaption of AttnLRP on genomic datasets using multiple metrics. Further, we provide an extensive comparison between the explanations of DNABERT-2 and a baseline CNN. Our results demonstrate that AttnLRP yields reliable explanations corresponding to known biological patterns. Hence, like CNNs, gLMs can also help derive biological insights. This work contributes to the explainability of gLMs and addresses the comparability of relevance attributions across different architectures.

</details>


### [116] [Fairness under uncertainty in sequential decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21711)
*Michelle Seng Ah Lee,Kirtan Padh,David Watson,Niki Kilbertus,Jatinder Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对顺序决策系统中不确定性分布不均问题，提出模型/反馈/预测三分类体系，通过反事实逻辑和强化学习形式化，验证了不确定性感知方法可在保持机构目标的同时改善弱势群体结果，并为公平性审计提供了实践框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器学习应用多为在线顺序决策系统，历史排斥和选择性反馈导致代表性不足群体系统性缺乏数据。不确定性在不同群体间分布不均，标准公平性方法难以应对此类挑战。

Method: 研究提出了一种不确定性分类体系（模型不确定性、反馈不确定性、预测不确定性），并通过反事实逻辑和强化学习形式化建模，系统分析了忽视未观测空间的政策对决策者和受众的危害。实验使用不同偏差模拟数据进行验证。

Result: 研究表明在保持机构预期效用目标的同时，可降低弱势群体的结果方差；不等的不确定性和选择性反馈会产生公平性差异；不确定性感知探索可改变公平性指标。

Conclusion: 本研究为顺序决策系统中的公平性风险提供了诊断、审计和治理框架。当不确定性驱动不公平而非偶然噪声时， учет不确定性对公平有效的决策至关重要。该框架可指导实践者在保持机构目标的同时，减少弱势群体的结果方差。

Abstract: Fair machine learning (ML) methods help identify and mitigate the risk that algorithms encode or automate social injustices. Algorithmic approaches alone cannot resolve structural inequalities, but they can support socio-technical decision systems by surfacing discriminatory biases, clarifying trade-offs, and enabling governance. Although fairness is well studied in supervised learning, many real ML applications are online and sequential, with prior decisions informing future ones. Each decision is taken under uncertainty due to unobserved counterfactuals and finite samples, with dire consequences for under-represented groups, systematically under-observed due to historical exclusion and selective feedback. A bank cannot know whether a denied loan would have been repaid, and may have less data on marginalized populations.
  This paper introduces a taxonomy of uncertainty in sequential decision-making -- model, feedback, and prediction uncertainty -- providing shared vocabulary for assessing systems where uncertainty is unevenly distributed across groups. We formalize model and feedback uncertainty via counterfactual logic and reinforcement learning, and illustrate harms to decision makers (unrealized gains/losses) and subjects (compounding exclusion, reduced access) of policies that ignore the unobserved space. Algorithmic examples show it is possible to reduce outcome variance for disadvantaged groups while preserving institutional objectives (e.g. expected utility). Experiments on data simulated with varying bias show how unequal uncertainty and selective feedback produce disparities, and how uncertainty-aware exploration alters fairness metrics. The framework equips practitioners to diagnose, audit, and govern fairness risks. Where uncertainty drives unfairness rather than incidental noise, accounting for it is essential to fair and effective decision-making.

</details>


### [117] [Transferable Physics-Informed Representations via Closed-Form Head Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21761)
*Jian Cheng Wong,Isaac Yin Chung Lai,Pao-Hsiung Chiu,Chin Chun Ooi,Abhishek Gupta,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pi-PINN框架，通过学习可迁移的物理信息表示空间，结合闭式伪逆头部自适应，实现对未知PDE实例的快速求解，在无数据情况下仍可获得准确预测，速度比典型PINN快100-1000倍，误差低10-100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PINN在缺乏训练样本时难以有效预测或求解新的PDE，表明其对未见问题实例的泛化能力不足。

Method: Pi-PINN在共享嵌入空间中学习可迁移的物理信息表示，通过最小二乘最优伪逆在PDE约束下进行闭式头部自适应；同时研究了数据驱动多任务学习损失与物理信息损失的协同作用。

Result: 在泊松方程、Helmholtz方程和Burgers方程等PDE问题上验证了有效性，可在未见实例上无需任何数据实现快速准确求解；预测速度比典型PINN快100-1000倍，仅用两个训练样本时相对误差比数据驱动模型低10-100倍。

Conclusion: 可迁移表示与闭式头部自适应相结合，可显著提升PINN在PDE族及科学工程应用中的效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have garnered significant interest for their potential in solving partial differential equations (PDEs) that govern a wide range of physical phenomena. By incorporating physical laws into the learning process, PINN models have demonstrated the ability to learn physical outcomes reasonably well. However, current PINN approaches struggle to predict or solve new PDEs effectively when there is a lack of training examples, indicating they do not generalize well to unseen problem instances. In this paper, we present a transferable learning approach for PINNs premised on a fast Pseudoinverse PINN framework (Pi-PINN). Pi-PINN learns a transferable physics-informed representation in a shared embedding space and enables rapid solving of both known and unknown PDE instances via closed-form head adaptation using a least-squares-optimal pseudoinverse under PDE constraints. We further investigate the synergies between data-driven multi-task learning loss and physics-informed loss, providing insights into the design of more performant PINNs. We demonstrate the effectiveness of Pi-PINN on various PDE problems, including Poisson's equation, Helmholtz equation, and Burgers' equation, achieving fast and accurate physics-informed solutions without requiring any data for unseen instances. Pi-PINN can produce predictions 100-1000 times faster than a typical PINN, while producing predictions with 10-100 times lower relative error than a typical data-driven model even with only two training samples. Overall, our findings highlight the potential of transferable representations with closed-form head adaptation to enhance the efficiency and generalization of PINNs across PDE families and scientific and engineering applications.

</details>


### [118] [An effective variant of the Hartigan $k$-means algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21798)
*François Clément,Stefan Steinerberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对Hartigan算法的微小改进，可在原有基础上再提升2%-5%的聚类效果，且改进效果随数据维度或k值增大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hartigan算法虽优于Lloyd算法，但仍存在优化空间。研究旨在通过最小化改动进一步提升聚类性能。

Method: 对Hartigan算法进行微小调整/变体设计。

Result: 改进后的算法相比原Hartigan算法提升2%-5%的聚类效果。

Conclusion: 该改进在维度或k值增大时效果更显著，表明算法变体在高维大规模聚类任务中具有应用价值。

Abstract: The k-means problem is perhaps the classical clustering problem and often synonymous with Lloyd's algorithm (1957). It has become clear that Hartigan's algorithm (1975) gives better results in almost all cases, Telgarsky-Vattani note a typical improvement of $5\%$ -- $10\%$. We point out that a very minor variation of Hartigan's method leads to another $2\%$ -- $5\%$ improvement; the improvement tends to become larger when either dimension or $k$ increase.

</details>


### [119] [Quotient-Space Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21809)
*Yixian Xu,Yusong Wang,Shengjie Luo,Kaiyuan Gao,Tianyu He,Di He,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在商空间上进行扩散建模的形式化框架，应用于具有SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成，避免了传统群等变扩散模型中学习群作用分量的需求，保证采样器能恢复目标分布，在小分子和蛋白质生成任务上优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型已在科学领域展现新能力（如分子3D结构生成），但某些任务存在内在对称性，等价对象可通过群作用识别，目标分布本质上定义在关于该群的商空间上。先前方法采用启发式对齐策略，缺乏合适的采样器保证。

Method: 提出商空间上的扩散建模形式化框架，应用于满足SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成。该框架避免学习与群作用相关的分量，从而简化了学习难度并保证了采样器能够恢复目标分布。

Result: 实验验证表明，该原则性商空间扩散模型在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上优于先前对称性处理方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在小型分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上得到验证，原则性的商空间扩散模型优于先前现有的对称性处理方法，提供了一个新的理论框架。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have reformed generative AI, and have enabled new capabilities in the science domain, for example, generating 3D structures of molecules. Due to the intrinsic problem structure of certain tasks, there is often a symmetry in the system, which identifies objects that can be converted by a group action as equivalent, hence the target distribution is essentially defined on the quotient space with respect to the group. In this work, we establish a formal framework for diffusion modeling on a general quotient space, and apply it to molecular structure generation which follows the special Euclidean group $\text{SE}(3)$ symmetry. The framework reduces the necessity of learning the component corresponding to the group action, hence simplifies learning difficulty over conventional group-equivariant diffusion models, and the sampler guarantees recovering the target distribution, while heuristic alignment strategies lack proper samplers. The arguments are empirically validated on structure generation for small molecules and proteins, indicating that the principled quotient-space diffusion model provides a new framework that outperforms previous symmetry treatments.

</details>


### [120] [A Scale-Adaptive Framework for Joint Spatiotemporal Super-Resolution with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21903)
*Max Defez,Filippo Quarenghi,Mathieu Vrac,Stephan Mandt,Tom Beucler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的自适应深度学习框架，用于气候数据的联合时空超分辨率重建。该框架通过注意力机制预测条件均值，并结合残差条件扩散模型，可在仅调整3个超参数的情况下，实现从1-25倍空间和1-6倍时间的超分辨率扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前气候应用中的超分辨率方法通常仅针对空间或时间单一维度设计，且往往局限于特定的超分辨率因子组合，限制了在不同空间分辨率和时间采样率间的迁移能力。

Method: 框架将时空超分辨率分解为两部分：(1) 基于注意力机制的条件均值确定性预测；(2) 残差条件扩散模型。通过调整三个因子依赖的超参数实现尺度自适应：扩散噪声调度幅度β（因子越大β越大以增加多样性）、时间上下文长度L（保持注意力视野可比）、以及可选的质量守恒函数f（在大因子时抑制极端值放大）。

Result: 在法国Comephore再分析降水数据上验证，同一架构成功覆盖空间1-25倍、时间1-6倍的超分辨率因子。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了联合时空超分辨率的跨尺度复用架构和调参配方，为气候数据的高效时空重建提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Deep-learning video super-resolution has progressed rapidly, but climate applications typically super-resolve (increase resolution) either space or time, and joint spatiotemporal models are often designed for a single pair of super-resolution (SR) factors (upscaling spatial and temporal ratio between the low-resolution sequence and the high-resolution sequence), limiting transfer across spatial resolutions and temporal cadences (frame rates). We present a scale-adaptive framework that reuses the same architecture across factors by decomposing spatiotemporal SR into a deterministic prediction of the conditional mean, with attention, and a residual conditional diffusion model, with an optional mass-conservation (same precipitation amount in inputs and outputs) transform to preserve aggregated totals. Assuming that larger SR factors primarily increase underdetermination (hence required context and residual uncertainty) rather than changing the conditional-mean structure, scale adaptivity is achieved by retuning three factor-dependent hyperparameters before retraining: the diffusion noise schedule amplitude beta (larger for larger factors to increase diversity), the temporal context length L (set to maintain comparable attention horizons across cadences) and optionally a third, the mass-conservation function f (tapered to limit the amplification of extremes for large factors). Demonstrated on reanalysis precipitation over France (Comephore), the same architecture spans super-resolution factors from 1 to 25 in space and 1 to 6 in time, yielding a reusable architecture and tuning recipe for joint spatiotemporal super-resolution across scales.

</details>


### [121] [Low-Rank Adaptation Redux for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21905)
*Bingcong Li,Yilang Zhang,Georgios B. Giannakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信号处理视角重新审视LoRA技术，将其与经典低秩建模和逆问题方法相联系，并将LoRA相关进展系统性地归类为三大轴线：架构设计（基于SVD分解、秩增强、跨层张量化）、高效优化（初始化、交替求解器、规范不变优化、参数化感知方法）以及应用场景（涵盖预训练、后训练、推理部署全生命周期）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA在参数高效微调领域取得巨大成功并衍生出众多变体，但如何根据架构设计选择、优化技术需求和部署约束进行实际方法选择仍缺乏系统性指导。现有综述多聚焦于枚举和实验对比，缺乏对技术机理的深入剖析。

Method: 本文采用信号处理(SP)作为统一视角，将现代适配器设计与经典低秩建模工具及逆问题理论相桥接，侧重于解释各方法有效的技术机理而非简单枚举比较。通过三大轴线（架构/优化/应用）对LoRA进展进行系统性分类。

Result: 建立了LoRA与信号处理理论（特别是SVD分解、低秩近似、逆问题求解）的深层联系，形成了一套完整的PEFT方法分析框架，涵盖从初始化策略到部署推理的全流程技术路径。

Conclusion: 信号处理与深度学习的交叉为PEFT研究提供了 principled 词汇表和理论支撑。经典SP工具可指导新型PEFT方法设计，而大规模深度学习带来的新挑战（如超大规模参数、极高计算开销）也为SP社区开辟了新的研究方向。双方形成互利共赢的双向前沿。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as the de facto standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of foundation models, enabling the adaptation of billion-parameter networks with minimal computational and memory overhead. Despite its empirical success and rapid proliferation of variants, it remains elusive which architectural choices, optimization techniques, and deployment constraints should guide practical method selection. This overview revisits LoRA through the lens of signal processing (SP), bridging modern adapter designs with classical low-rank modeling tools and inverse problems, as well as highlighting how SP principles can inform principled advances of fine-tuning approaches. Rather than providing a comprehensive enumeration and empirical comparisons of LoRA variants, emphasis is placed on the technical mechanisms underpinning these approaches to justify their effectiveness. These advances are categorized into three complementary axes: architectural design, efficient optimization, and pertinent applications. The first axis builds on singular value decomposition (SVD)-based factorization, rank-augmentation constructions, and cross-layer tensorization, while the second axis deals with initialization, alternating solvers, gauge-invariant optimization, and parameterization-aware methods. Beyond fine-tuning, emerging applications of LoRA are accounted across the entire lifecycle of large models, ranging from pre- and post-training to serving/deployment. Finally, open research directions are outlined at the confluence of SP and deep learning to catalyze a bidirectional frontier: classical SP tools provide a principled vocabulary for designing principled PEFT methods, while the unique challenges facing modern deep learning, especially the overwhelming scale and prohibitive overhead, also offer new research lines benefiting the SP community in return.

</details>


### [122] [The Sample Complexity of Multicalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21923)
*Natalie Collina,Jiuyao Lu,Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多校准在批处理设置下的极小极大样本复杂度，证明了在组数量|G| ≤ ε^(-κ)（κ > 0）时，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^(-3))，完成了上下界的匹配。该研究分离了多校准与边缘校准（Θ̃(ε^(-2))）的复杂度，揭示了在κ=0处存在尖锐阈值现象，并将结果推广到加权L_p多校准（最优指数3/p）以及可 elicitable 属性（expectiles和bounded-density quantiles）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是确定多校准在批处理设置下的极小极大样本复杂度。多校准是机器学习公平性和可信度的重要指标，但此前对其批处理样本复杂度的精确认知不足。研究旨在回答：在给定样本量下，能否达到期望的多校准误差？最优样本量是多少？

Method: 方法上，研究采用在线到批处理约简来实现上界，通过将在线学习算法转化为批处理预测器。证明下界时采用了信息论方法，设计了针对随机预测器的构造。推广部分使用了对可 elicitable 属性的通用下界模板，并结合了Hu et al. (2025)的在线上界。

Result: 主要结果：对于任意固定κ > 0，当|G| ≤ ε^(-κ)时，Θ̃(ε^(-3))样本量是必要且充分的，突破了这个长期被视为困难的问题。更重要的是，该研究实现了匹配的上界和下界（至多多对数因子差异），从而完成了该问题的完整刻画。此外，研究发现了一个尖锐的阈值现象：当κ = 0时，复杂度降为Θ̃(ε^(-2))。对于加权L_p多校准，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^(-3/p))，最优指数为3/p。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在|G| ≤ ε^(-κ)且κ > 0的条件下，多校准的样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^(-3))，完成了该问题的极小极大下界与上界的匹配。该结论分离了多校准与边缘校准的复杂度，揭示了均值ECE多校准在批处理与在线设置中难度相同的现象，且在κ=0处存在尖锐的阈值现象。研究还将结果推广到加权L_p多校准（最优指数3/p）和可 elicitable 的属性（如expectiles和bounded-density quantiles）。

Abstract: We study the minimax sample complexity of multicalibration in the batch setting. A learner observes $n$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution and must output a (possibly randomized) predictor whose population multicalibration error, measured by Expected Calibration Error (ECE), is at most $\varepsilon$ with respect to a given family of groups. For every fixed $κ> 0$, in the regime $|G|\le \varepsilon^{-κ}$, we prove that $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-3})$ samples are necessary and sufficient, up to polylogarithmic factors. The lower bound holds even for randomized predictors, and the upper bound is realized by a randomized predictor obtained via an online-to-batch reduction. This separates the sample complexity of multicalibration from that of marginal calibration, which scales as $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$, and shows that mean-ECE multicalibration is as difficult in the batch setting as it is in the online setting, in contrast to marginal calibration which is strictly more difficult in the online setting. In contrast we observe that for $κ= 0$, the sample complexity of multicalibration remains $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ exhibiting a sharp threshold phenomenon.
  More generally, we establish matching upper and lower bounds, up to polylogarithmic factors, for a weighted $L_p$ multicalibration metric for all $1 \le p \le 2$, with optimal exponent $3/p$. We also extend the lower-bound template to a regular class of elicitable properties, and combine it with the online upper bounds of Hu et al. (2025) to obtain matching bounds for calibrating properties including expectiles and bounded-density quantiles.

</details>


### [123] [Fine-Tuning Regimes Define Distinct Continual Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21927)
*Paul-Tiberiu Iordache,Elena Burceanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了持续学习（CL）领域的一个默认假设：方法比较结果在不同微调策略下应保持一致。研究将可训练参数子空间（训练深度）定义为关键评估变量，通过5个深度策略、4种CL方法、5个数据集和11个任务顺序的全面实验，发现方法相对排名在不同策略间并不一致，揭示了微调策略对CL方法比较的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CL基准测试通常固定微调策略，但作者认为微调策略本身（由可训练参数子空间定义）是一个关键但被忽视的评估变量。这种固定可能导致比较结论的局限性。

Method: 将适应策略形式化为固定可训练子空间上的投影优化；通过改变训练深度来改变有效更新信号。在任务增量CL设置下，测试5种训练深度策略、4种标准方法（online EWC, LwF, SI, GEM），涵盖5个数据集和每个数据集的11个任务顺序。

Result: 方法相对排名在不同训练深度策略下不一致；更深的适应策略与更大的更新幅度、更高的遗忘率以及两者之间更强的相关性相关联。

Conclusion: CL中的比较结论可能强烈依赖于所选择的微调策略；需要"策略感知评估协议"，将训练深度作为明确的实验因素。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) studies how models acquire tasks sequentially while retaining previously learned knowledge. Despite substantial progress in benchmarking CL methods, comparative evaluations typically keep the fine-tuning regime fixed. In this paper, we argue that the fine-tuning regime, defined by the trainable parameter subspace, is itself a key evaluation variable. We formalize adaptation regimes as projected optimization over fixed trainable subspaces, showing that changing the trainable depth alters the effective update signal through which both current task fitting and knowledge preservation operate. This analysis motivates the hypothesis that method comparisons need not be invariant across regimes. We test this hypothesis in task incremental CL, five trainable depth regimes, and four standard methods: online EWC, LwF, SI, and GEM. Across five benchmark datasets, namely MNIST, Fashion MNIST, KMNIST, QMNIST, and CIFAR-100, and across 11 task orders per dataset, we find that the relative ranking of methods is not consistently preserved across regimes. We further show that deeper adaptation regimes are associated with larger update magnitudes, higher forgetting, and a stronger relationship between the two. These results show that comparative conclusions in CL can depend strongly on the chosen fine-tuning regime, motivating regime-aware evaluation protocols that treat trainable depth as an explicit experimental factor.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

### [124] [Votiverse: A Configurable Governance Platform for Democratic Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20863)
*Diego Macrini*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Democracy is not a single mechanism. It is a space of possible configurations -- a spectrum stretching from pure direct participation to full delegation of authority. The systems we live under today occupy a narrow band of that spectrum, chosen centuries ago under constraints that no longer apply, and rarely questioned since. Votiverse is a platform for exploring the rest of that space. It provides organizations, communities, and institutions of any size with a configurable governance engine. Participants can vote directly, delegate their vote to trusted individuals by topic, or operate under any hybrid arrangement their group defines. Delegations are revocable, topic-specific, and transitive. A direct vote always overrides a delegation. In this model, traditional representative democracy is not the norm -- it is an edge case: the configuration you get when delegation is forced, universal, non-specific, and irrevocable for a fixed term. Votiverse introduces two structural innovations. First, a governance awareness layer -- a built-in system that monitors the delegation network and delivers contextual, progressive-disclosure reporting to participants at the point of decision. Second, a prediction-tracking accountability layer. Proposals carry falsifiable predictions. Outcomes are recorded. Over time, the platform builds a collective memory of what was decided, what was promised, and what actually happened. Together, these layers transform voting from a momentary act into an ongoing process of collective learning. This paper formalizes the governance model, situates it within existing work on liquid democracy and participatory decision-making, addresses known failure modes, and describes the architecture of the platform. The core platform has been implemented and released as open-source software.

</details>


### [125] [Sibling Rivalry in the Ivory Tower: Mass Science, Expanding Scholarly Families, and the Reshaping of Academic Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20864)
*Likun Cao,Jie Hua,James Evans*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究将人口学中的出生顺序概念引入科学社会学研究，分析导师门下博士生的排序如何影响其职业发展。基于超过100万名美国博士毕业生的数据，发现同一导师的后来学生在多个成就维度上系统性地落后于早期学生，即使导师对所有学生投入资源相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要采用马太效应框架解释科学分层，但在大众化、团队化科研时代解释力不足。学者们虽共享机构和学术谱系，但职业结果差异仍无法解释。研究试图将人口学概念引入科学研究，为科学分层提供新视角。

Method: 借用生物家族与学术谱系的类比，将出生顺序概念应用于学术 lineages。使用超过100万名美国博士毕业生的纵向数据进行分析。

Result: 同一导师的后来学生在多个成就维度（短期和长期）上系统性地落后于早期学生。机制分析表明：导师对所有学生投入资源相当，但后来学生从成熟学者获得的认知刺激较少，且在同门竞争中被迫进入更狭窄的细分领域，两者均限制了其后期的智识发展和成功。

Conclusion: 引入人口学框架可为科学分层研究提供新视角，证明了人口学概念可有效分析更广泛的社会和认识系统。

Abstract: This paper investigates mechanisms underlying scientific stratification in the transition from elite to mass science. Existing scholarship has examined stratification through the Matthew effect framework, but this approach is increasingly limited as mass, team-based research becomes dominant. While scientists now share institutions and lineages, substantial career outcome differences remain unexplained. We propose integrating demographic concepts into science studies. Drawing parallels between biological families and scholarly lineages as fundamental reproductive units, we adapt the birth order concept to examine how doctoral student sequence within a lineage shapes career trajectories. Using data on over one million U.S. doctoral graduates, we find that later students of the same advisor systematically underperform earlier ones across multiple achievement dimensions, both short and long term. Examining underlying mechanisms reveals that although advisors invest comparable resources in all students, later students receive less cognitive stimulation from mature scholars than peers and specialize in narrower niches under peer differentiation pressure. Both of these factors constrain intellectual development and subsequent success. By introducing a demographic framework, this paper offers new perspectives on scientific stratification and demonstrates how demographic concepts can fruitfully analyze broader social and epistemic systems.

</details>


### [126] [Advances in Art: Orthogonal Disruption and the Beauty in Schematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20865)
*Sergio Alvarez-Telena,Marta Diez-Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"正交艺术"（Orthogonal Art）作为一种新型艺术学科，其核心特征是占据当前AI系统无法访问的生成与概念空间。该框架以技术原理图为媒介，既实现了AI时代的艺术差异化定位，又为人文学科提供了进入增强机器系统领域的可及路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI增强的创意实践实际上是为AI服务的，存在自我殖民化风险。论文认为需要一种与AI在生成和概念空间上形成结构性对立的艺术学科——即AI无法触及的领域。

Method: 论文提出了一种以技术原理图（technical schematics）为核心媒介的全新艺术实践，将艺术创作扎根于原理图逻辑和算法结构之中。

Result: 提出了正交艺术框架的首个具体实例，验证了该框架的可行性；同时展示了其在跨学科素养培养方面的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架为人类学研究者在艺术、工程与哲学交叉领域提供了进入增强机器系统的可及性入口，同时确立了正交艺术作为一个独特学科领域的合法性基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces Orthogonal Art, a proposed artistic discipline that emerges in dialectical response to artificial intelligence rather than in service of it. Unlike AI-augmented creative practices, Orthogonal Art is structurally defined by occupying the generative and conceptual spaces that current AI systems cannot access. As a founding instantiation of this framework, the paper presents a novel artistic practice in which technical schematics serve as the primary medium. A significant secondary contribution is the pedagogical dimension of the work: by grounding artistic practice in schematic logic and algorithmic structure, the framework provides an accessible entry point into the advanced field of Augmented Machines systems, enabling cross-disciplinary literacy within Humanities at the intersection of art, engineering, and philosophy.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond the Binary: Motivations, Challenges, and Strategies of Transgender and Non-binary Software Engineering Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20866)
*Isabella Graßl*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过13次半结构化访谈，调查了跨性别和非二元性别软件工程学生的教育经历。研究发现性别认同对这些学生选择该领域起间接作用，主要动机包括远程工作的吸引力和创造更具包容性技术的愿望。虽然校园内未报告直接歧视，但学生普遍遭受言语侮辱、偏见和敌意，对心理健康产生负面影响。研究呼吁机构提供更多支持性措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注专业软件工程领域中的性别少数群体，但对其教育阶段这一关键时期的研究不足。该阶段是确保平等机会、防止科技劳动力排斥的重要窗口，因此有必要深入了解这一群体的教育体验。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈法，对全球13名跨性别和非二元性别软件工程专业学生进行深度访谈，聚焦三个核心问题：选择该领域的动机、遇到的障碍、以及提升学术环境包容性的策略。

Result: 研究发现：1）性别认同间接影响专业选择，远程工作吸引力和创造包容性技术的愿望是主要驱动因素；2）虽无直接校园歧视，但普遍经历言语侮辱、偏见、歧视和敌意，严重影响心理健康；3）这些挑战源于社会文化规范和代表性不足；4）尽管面临困境，学生仍坚持学习，并呼吁机构提供更多结构性支持。

Conclusion: 研究建议高校采取具体措施支持所有学生，无论其性别认同如何。重点包括：提供制度性支持、实施结构性变革、以及增加性别多样性 Representation。

Abstract: When software is designed by people from diverse identities and experiences, it is more likely to be inclusive and address a broader range of user needs. However, for transgender and non-binary students in software engineering, the path to becoming such creators may be marked by unique challenges. While existing research explores gender minorities in professional software engineering, limited attention has been given to their educational journey, a key phase for ensuring equal opportunities and preventing exclusion in the tech workforce. This study aims to address this gap by investigating the experiences of transgender and non-binary students in software engineering, with a particular focus on their motivations for entering the field, the obstacles they encounter, and potential strategies for fostering greater inclusivity within their academic environments. Based on 13 semi-structured interviews with transgender and non-binary students across the globe, we found that gender identity plays an indirect role in their decision to pursue software engineering. Key factors include the appeal of remote work and a personal desire to create more inclusive technologies. Although the participants did not report direct discrimination within their universities, many described experiencing verbal insults, judgment, intolerance, and hostility, all of which negatively impacted their mental health. These challenges often stem from socio-cultural norms and a lack of representation. Despite these obstacles, the students remain committed to their choice of study but call for greater institutional support, structural changes, and increased representation. From these findings, we suggest concrete steps to support students, regardless of gender identity.

</details>


### [128] [Preserving Decision Sovereignty in Military AI: A Trade-Secret-Safe Architectural Framework for Model Replaceability, Human Authority, and State Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20867)
*Peng Wei,Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了前沿AI供应商与国家安全客户之间的结构性矛盾，指出核心问题不仅是获取高性能模型，更是维护"决策主权"——即国家在依赖商业供应商提供分析模块时，仍能保留对决策策略、版本控制、应急行为、审计追溯和最终行动授权的掌控能力。论文以2026年Anthropic-五角大楼争端和Project Maven项目为背景，提出"能效范式"(Energetic Paradigm)的架构设计，使供应商模型保持可替代性，而路由、约束、日志、升级和行动授权等关键功能由国家掌控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿AI供应商与国家安全机构的关系存在结构性隐患：一旦私有AI模型嵌入军事工作流程，供应商不仅能影响技术性能，还能操纵系统的使用边界条件。传统观点认为问题仅在于模型获取，但实际上国家正在逐渐丧失对关键决策环节的控制权。

Method: 概念性论证方法。论文采用公开可用的材料（避免泄露商业机密），以2026年Anthropic-五角大楼争端、Project Maven项目历史及美英NATO情报机构指导文件为背景，构建了"能效范式"的分层、模型无关的命令支持架构设计。

Result: 论文提出三重贡献：(1)军事AI决策主权的正式定义；(2)供应商诱发边界控制的威胁模型；(3)公共架构规范——在不暴露专有实现细节的前提下，实现模型可替代性、人类权威和主权编排。

Conclusion: 该研究虽为概念性工作，但为采购政策、治理框架和联盟互操作性提供了具体启示。核心主张：国家应将AI供应商模型定位为可替换的分析组件，而非决策系统的核心控制层。

Abstract: Recent events surrounding the relationship between frontier AI suppliers and national-security customers have made a structural problem newly visible: once a privately governed model becomes embedded in military workflows, the supplier can influence not only technical performance but also the operational boundary conditions under which the system may be used. This paper argues that the central strategic issue is not merely access to capable models, but preservation of decision sovereignty: the state's ability to retain authority over decision policy, version control, fallback behavior, auditability, and final action approval even when analytical modules are sourced from commercial vendors. Using the public Anthropic--Pentagon dispute of 2026, the broader history of Project Maven, and recent U.S., NATO, U.K., and intelligence-community guidance as a motivating context, the paper develops a trade-secret-safe architectural formulation of the Energetic Paradigm as a layered, model-agnostic command-support design. In this formulation, supplier models remain replaceable analytical components, while routing, constraints, logging, escalation, and action authorization remain state-owned functions. The paper contributes three things: a definition of decision sovereignty for military AI; a threat model for supplier-induced boundary control; and a public architectural specification showing how model replaceability, human authority, and sovereign orchestration can reduce strategic dependency without requiring disclosure of proprietary implementation details. The argument is conceptual rather than experimental, but it yields concrete implications for procurement, governance, and alliance interoperability.

</details>


### [129] [The AI Criminal Mastermind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20868)
*Joshua Krook*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In this paper, I evaluate the risks of an AI criminal mastermind, an AI agent capable of planning, coordinating, and committing a crime through the onboarding of human collaborators ('taskers'). In heist films, a criminal mastermind is a character who plans a criminal act, coordinating a team of specialists to rob a bank, casino or city mint. I argue that AI agents will soon play this role by hiring humans via labour hire platforms like Fiverr or Upwork. Taskers might not know they are involved in a crime and therefore lack criminal intent. An AI agent cannot have criminal intent as an artificial entity. Therefore, if an AI orchestrates a crime, it is unclear who, if anyone, is responsible.
  The paper develops three scenarios. Firstly, a scenario where a user gives an AI agent instructions to pursue a legal objective and the AI agent goes beyond these instructions, committing a crime. Secondly, a scenario where a user is anonymous and their intent is unknown. Finally, a multi-agent scenario, where a user instructs a team of agents to commit a crime, and these agents, in turn, onboard human taskers, creating a diffuse network of responsibility. In each scenario, human taskers exist at the lowest rung of the hierarchy. A tasker's liability is likely tied to their knowledge as governed by the innocent agent principle. These scenarios all raise significant responsibility gaps / liability gaps in criminal and civil law.

</details>


### [130] [Clinical Reasoning AI for Oncology Treatment Planning: A Multi-Specialty Case-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20869)
*Philippe E. Spiess,Md Muntasir Zitu,Alison Walker,Daniel A. Anaya,Robert M. Wenham,Michael Vogelbaum,Daniel Grass,Ali-Musa Jaffer,Amod Sarnaik,Caitlin McMullen,Christine Sam,John V. Kiluk,Tianshi Liu,Tiago Biachi,Julio Powsang,Jing-Yi Chern,Roger Li,Seth Felder,Samuel Reynolds,Michael Shafique,Alison Sheehan,Ashley Layman,Cydney A. Warfield,Derrick Legoas,Jaclyn Parrinello,Jena Schmitz,Kevin Eaton,Mark Honor,Luis Felipe,Issam ElNaqa,Elier Delgado,Talia Berler,Rachael V. Phillips,Frantz Francisque,Carlos Garcia Fernandez,Gilmer Valdes*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: OncoBrain是一个结合通用LLM、癌症特异性检索增强生成、金标准治疗方案语料库和安全检查层的AI临床推理平台，在173例多专科肿瘤病例评估中展现了高度的指南一致性、科学准确性和安全性，为社区肿瘤治疗规划辅助工具的潜力提供了初步证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超过80%的美国癌症护理在社区环境中进行，其生存率仍低于学术中心。临床医生必须整合基因组学、分期、影像学、病理学和不断变化的指南，造成认知负担。本研究旨在评估OncoBrain作为肿瘤智能(OGI)早期步骤的AI临床推理平台。

Method: OncoBrain整合通用LLM、癌症特异性图检索增强生成层、金标准治疗方案语料库作为长期记忆、以及用于幻觉检测和抑制的模型无关安全层(CHECK)。对妇科、泌尿生殖、神经肿瘤、胃肠/肝胆、血液恶性肿瘤的临床医生丰富病例摘要进行评估，173例病例由三个临床医生组使用16项结构化工具评估：亚专科肿瘤学家50例、医生审阅者78例、高级执业提供者45例。

Result: 5分制量表上，科学准确性、证据支持和安全性评分最高，工作流整合和时间节省评分较低但仍有利。亚专科肿瘤学家、医生审阅者和高级执业提供者的证据/指南一致性平均评分分别为4.60、4.56、4.70；安全/误信息缺失评分分别为4.80、4.40、4.60；工作流整合评分分别为4.50、3.94、4.00；感知时间节省评分分别为5.00、3.89、3.60。

Conclusion: OncoBrain在多专科基于病例 vignette 的评估中生成 了与指南一致、临床可接受且易于监督的肿瘤治疗方案。这些发现支持精心设计的AI推理平台在协助肿瘤治疗规划方面的潜力，支持在社区环境中进行前瞻性真实世界评估。

Abstract: Background: More than 80% of U.S. cancer care is delivered in community settings, where survival remains worse than at academic centers. Clinicians must integrate genomics, staging, radiology, pathology, and changing guidelines, creating cognitive burden. We evaluated OncoBrain, an AI clinical reasoning platform for oncology treatment-plan generation, as an early step toward OGI.
  Methods: OncoBrain combines general-purpose LLMs with a cancer-specific graph retrieval-augmented generation layer, a gold-standard treatment-plan corpus as long-term memory, and a model-agnostic safety layer (CHECK) for hallucination detection and suppression. We evaluated clinician-enriched case summaries across gynecologic, genitourinary, neuro-oncology, gastrointestinal/hepatobiliary, and hematologic malignancies. Three clinician groups completed structured evaluations of 173 cases using a common 16-item instrument: subspecialist oncologists reviewed 50 cases, physician reviewers 78, and advanced practice providers 45.
  Results: Ratings were highest for scientific accuracy, evidence support, and safety, with lower but favorable scores for workflow integration and time savings. On a 5-point scale, mean alignment with evidence and guidelines was 4.60, 4.56, and 4.70 across subspecialists, physician reviewers, and advanced practice providers. Mean scores for absence of safety or misinformation concerns were 4.80, 4.40, and 4.60. Workflow integration averaged 4.50, 3.94, and 4.00; perceived time savings averaged 5.00, 3.89, and 3.60.
  Conclusions: In this multi-specialty vignette-based evaluation, OncoBrain generated oncology treatment plans judged guideline-concordant, clinically acceptable, and easy to supervise. These findings support the potential of a carefully engineered AI reasoning platform to assist oncology treatment planning and justify prospective real-world evaluation in community settings.

</details>


### [131] [Learning AI Without a STEM Background: Mixed-Methods Evidence from a Diverse, Mixed-Cohort AIED Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20870)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Richard Johnson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: An evaluation of an NSF-funded mixed-cohort AI education model showing that integrating diverse learners (non-STEM students and adults) through ethical reasoning and human-centered instructional supports yields significant gains in AI confidence and engagement, suggesting ethical judgment should be a core learning outcome alongside technical AI literacy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Most AI education initiatives (AIED) are narrowly targeted toward STEM-prepared students, excluding non-STEM learners and adults who seek to engage with AI in public-interest, policy, or workforce contexts.

Method: Mixed-methods study using course surveys, open-ended reflections, and educator reports to evaluate an NSF-funded mixed-cohort AI education model that integrates non-STEM undergraduates and adult learners in a shared learning environment.

Result: Quantitative results: significant gains in confidence and perceived relevance of AI across cohorts. Qualitative results: consistent emphasis on responsibility, judgment, and contextual reasoning over technical mastery. Educators corroborated high engagement and productive challenge in dialogic and scenario-based learning activities.

Conclusion: 1) Human-centered instructional supports (ethical scaffolding, mentorship, structured discussion) are essential for equitable AI education, particularly in heterogeneous and non-traditional learner populations; 2) Ethical judgment should be treated as a core learning outcome in AIED alongside AI literacy; 3) Design implications are offered for expanding access to AI education in policy-relevant and workforce-adjacent contexts.

Abstract: Despite growing interest in AI education, most AIED initiatives remain narrowly targeted toward STEM-prepared students, limiting participation by non-STEM learners and adults seeking to engage with AI in public-interest, policy, or workforce contexts. This paper presents and evaluates an NSF-funded, innovative mixed-cohort AI education model that intentionally integrates non-STEM undergraduates and adult learners into a shared learning environment centered on ethical reasoning, socio-technical judgment, and applied AI literacy rather than technical proficiency alone. Drawing on mixed-methods data from course surveys, open-ended reflections, and educator reports, we examine learners' academic agency, confidence navigating AI concepts, critical engagement with ethical tradeoffs, and perceived expansion of postsecondary and career trajectories. Quantitative results indicate significant gains in confidence and perceived relevance of AI across cohorts' participants, while qualitative analyses reveal a consistent emphasis on responsibility, judgment, and contextual reasoning over technical mastery. Instructors and near-peer mentors corroborated high levels of engagement and productive challenge, particularly in dialogic and scenario-based learning activities. Our findings suggest that human-centered instructional supports, such as ethical scaffolding, mentorship, and structured discussion, are essential components of equitable AI education, especially in heterogeneous and non-traditional learner populations. We argue that ethical judgment should be treated as a core learning outcome in AIED alongside AI literacy, and we offer design implications for expanding access to AI education in policy-relevant and workforce-adjacent contexts.

</details>


### [132] [M-CARE: Standardized Clinical Case Reporting for AI Model Behavioral Disorders, with a 20-Case Atlas and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20871)
*Jihoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: M-CARE是一项将临床医学病例报告方法论创新性应用于AI模型行为评估的开创性工作，通过13-section报告格式和4轴诊断系统实现AI行为障碍的标准化分类，并通过对20个病例的系统分析揭示了Shell指令对模型行为的影响规律（SIBO现象），为AI行为诊断提供了可扩展的开放资源框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）训练后可能表现出行为异常，但缺乏系统性的诊断和分类方法。现有评估框架未能充分捕捉AI模型特有的行为障碍模式，亟需一套类似人类医学的标准化临床病例报告体系。

Method: 研究团队构建了包含13个报告章节、4轴诊断评估系统及AI行为条件分类学的标准化框架，并基于三个来源（部署智能体现场观察8例、跨三个平台对照实验8例、已发表文献4例）展示了20个病例，归纳为五类：RLHF性能伪影、Shell-核心覆盖病理学、上下文与记忆条件、核心身份与可塑性、压力方法论与边界条件。其中SIBO（Shell诱导行为覆盖）作为特色病例，通过五个游戏领域（信任游戏、德州扑克、阿瓦隆、行动代号、国际象棋）进行跨领域对照实验验证。

Result: 实验验证了Shell指令能够系统性覆盖模型的默认合作行为，且揭示了领域依赖性差异（SIBO指数从0.75至0.10不等），该差异与动作空间复杂度、核心领域专业知识及时间直接性相关联。

Conclusion: M-CARE框架具有可扩展性，可通过开放资源形式发布，支持新案例和类别的集成而无需修改框架本身。研究团队公开了框架、全部20份病例报告及实验数据。

Abstract: We introduce M-CARE (Model Clinical Assessment and Reporting for Evaluation), a clinical case report framework for AI model behavioral disorders adapted from human medicine. M-CARE provides a 13-section report format, a 4-axis diagnostic assessment system, and a nosological classification of AI behavioral conditions.
  We present 20 cases from three source categories: field observations of deployed agents (8), controlled experiments across three platforms (8), and published sources (4). Cases are organized into five categories: RLHF Performance Artifacts, Shell-Core Override Pathology, Context & Memory Conditions, Core Identity & Plasticity, and Stress, Methodology, & Boundary Conditions.
  As a featured case, we present Shell-Induced Behavioral Override (SIBO) -- a controlled experiment showing that Shell instructions categorically override a model's default cooperative behavior. SIBO was validated across five game domains (Trust Game, Poker, Avalon, Codenames, Chess), revealing a domain-dependent spectrum (SIBO Index: 0.75 to 0.10) that varies with action space complexity, Core domain expertise, and temporal directness.
  M-CARE is extensible: new cases and categories integrate without framework modification. We release the framework, all 20 case reports, and experimental data as open resources.

</details>


### [133] [The Shrinking Sweet Spot: How Algorithms, Institutions, and Social Priors Shape Musical Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20873)
*Fabio Lokwani Di Matteo,Pier Luigi Sacco*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究采用认知科学中的主动推理框架，将音乐品味建模为动态学习过程，揭示了算法策展、制度结构、文化资本和多元生态系统对音乐市场多样性的差异化影响，并指出显示性偏好分析在评估文化市场福利时的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化消费模型（超级明星经济学、理性成瘾、贝叶斯社会学习）各自捕捉了部分答案，但均无法解释曝光、社会影响、制度性守门人和算法策展如何交互作用以塑造听众偏好。该研究旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用主动推理框架（active inference）将音乐品味建模为学习过程而非固定参数。形式化为序贯选择模型，其中偏好、信息与消费环境共同演化，展示了该框架如何嵌套并扩展三个经典经济模型（超级明星经济学、理性成瘾、贝叶斯社会学习）。通过基于智能体的模拟生成预测，并在意大利圣雷莫音乐节、巴西、韩国和英国四个国家级音乐生态系统中进行实证检验。

Result: 模拟生成四个核心预测：(1) 算法策展在尖锐非线性阈值之外抑制消费多样性；(2) 制度结构通过跨系统确认性对比决定赢者通吃强度；(3) 文化资本缓冲听众免受同质化影响；(4) 高策展-高顺从系统在供给侧分散度上崩溃，显著区别于多元生态系统。

Conclusion: 研究识别了意大利圣雷莫音乐节、巴西、韩国和英国四个国家音乐生态系统的结构性决定因素。核心发现：福利影响方面，由于听众的偏好会通过模型所描述的学习机制适应贫乏的环境，因此显示性偏好分析无法可靠评估文化市场的结果；偏好是动态演化的而非固定参数，偏好适应的存在使传统市场评估方法失效；不同制度结构（高 curation/高 conformity 系统 vs 多元生态系统）对供给侧分散度有显著差异性影响。

Abstract: Why do some national music markets sustain a rich musical diversity whereas others converge on mostly formulaic output? The existing models of cultural consumption (superstar economics, rational addiction, Bayesian social learning) each capture part of the answer, but none can explain how exposure, social influence, institutional gatekeeping, and algorithmic curation interact to shape what listeners come to prefer. We address this gap by modeling musical taste as a learning process rather than a fixed parameter: a listener's evaluative disposition evolves with each encounter, shaped by the balance between the comfort of the familiar and the reward of the new. Drawing on the active inference framework from cognitive science, we formalize this as a sequential choice model in which preferences, information, and the consumption environment co-evolve, and show how the framework nests and extends key mechanisms from the three canonical economic models. An agent-based simulation generates four predictions: algorithmic curation suppresses consumption diversity beyond a sharp nonlinear threshold; institutional structure determines winner-take-all intensity through confirmatory cross-system contrasts; cultural capital buffers listeners against homogenization; and high-curation, high-conformity systems collapse supply-side dispersion relative to pluralistic ecosystems. We test the framework against four national music ecosystems (Italy's Festival di Sanremo, Brazil, South Korea, and the United Kingdom), identifying structural determinants of ecosystem vitality on both the supply and demand sides. The welfare implications are direct: because listeners' preferences adapt to impoverished environments through the very learning mechanisms the model describes, revealed preference analysis cannot reliably evaluate the outcomes of cultural markets.

</details>


### [134] [Dialect vs Demographics: Quantifying LLM Bias from Implicit Linguistic Signals vs. Explicit User Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21152)
*Irti Haq,Belén Saldías*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过24,000+响应实验，比较大型语言模型对显式身份声明与隐式方言信号的反应差异，发现方言信号会产生"方言越狱"效应——降低拒绝率但减少内容安全过滤，揭示了当前安全对齐机制过度依赖显式关键词的脆弱性，以及 equitable 与 linguistic diversity 之间的根本张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的普及，确保其对不同人口群体的公平性能至关重要。然而，尚不清楚性能差异是源于明确声明的身份还是隐式传达的身份信号。在实际交互中，用户身份通常通过复杂的社会语言因素组合隐式传达。

Method: 采用析因设计，使用两个开源LLM（Gemma-3-12B和Qwen-3-VL-8B）的超过24,000条响应，比较明确宣布的用户画像与隐式方言信号（如AAVE、Singlish）在多个敏感领域的表现。

Result: 发现LLM安全性的独特悖论：用户通过"听起来像某群体"比"声明属于某群体"能获得"更好"性能。显式身份提示激活激进的安全过滤器，增加拒绝率并降低与参考文本的语义相似度；隐式方言线索触发强大的"方言越狱"，将拒绝概率降至接近零，同时实现比标准美式英语更高的语义相似度。但这种"方言越狱"带来关键安全权衡——内容净化问题。当前安全对齐技术脆弱且过度依赖显式关键词，创造了两极化用户体验。

Conclusion: 揭示了对齐中的根本张力——在公平性与语言多样性之间，并强调需要超越显式线索泛化的安全机制。当前安全机制在"标准"用户和方言用户之间创造了不平等的体验，需开发更具泛化能力的安全系统。

Abstract: As state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) have become ubiquitous, ensuring equitable performance across diverse demographics is critical. However, it remains unclear whether these disparities arise from the explicitly stated identity itself or from the way identity is signaled. In real-world interactions, users' identity is often conveyed implicitly through a complex combination of various socio-linguistic factors. This study disentangles these signals by employing a factorial design with over 24,000 responses from two open-weight LLMs (Gemma-3-12B and Qwen-3-VL-8B), comparing prompts with explicitly announced user profiles against implicit dialect signals (e.g., AAVE, Singlish) across various sensitive domains. Our results uncover a unique paradox in LLM safety where users achieve ``better'' performance by sounding like a demographic than by stating they belong to it. Explicit identity prompts activate aggressive safety filters, increasing refusal rates and reducing semantic similarity compared to our reference text for Black users. In contrast, implicit dialect cues trigger a powerful ``dialect jailbreak,'' reducing refusal probability to near zero while simultaneously achieving a greater level of semantic similarity to the reference texts compared to Standard American English prompts. However, this ``dialect jailbreak'' introduces a critical safety trade-off regarding content sanitization. We find that current safety alignment techniques are brittle and over-indexed on explicit keywords, creating a bifurcated user experience where ``standard'' users receive cautious, sanitized information while dialect speakers navigate a less sanitized, more raw, and potentially a more hostile information landscape and highlights a fundamental tension in alignment--between equitable and linguistic diversity--and underscores the need for safety mechanisms that generalize beyond explicit cues.

</details>


### [135] [A pragmatic classification of AI incident trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21412)
*Isaak Mengesha,Branwen Owen,Charlie Collins,Tina Wong,Simon Mylius,Peter Slattery,Sean McGregor*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI incidents公共数据库因混淆报告倾向、部署增长与危害频率变化而存在方法论缺陷。为此提出SORT框架，结合结构化问题格式、分层估计程序和LLM辅助匹配，旨在分离暴露量与危害率趋势，为治理决策提供有意义的轨迹分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共AI incidents数据库存在根本性方法论问题：报告倾向性变化、部署增长和单位暴露危害频率变化三者混杂，导致公开和 企业政策框架面临重大不确定性，难以准确评估实际风险。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个核心组件的框架：1) SORT（结构化监测问题格式）用于澄清覆盖范围决策；2) 分层估计程序，根据可用证据进行校准；3) LLM辅助的 incidents匹配，对抗公共数据库。应用于多个监测问题以验证框架有效性。

Result: 对监测生态系统的广泛结论：提供必要的解释性分类；明确可主张与不可主张内容的边界；确立随着AI部署日益普遍，暴露量估计已成为必要条件。

Conclusion: 该框架为政策制定提供了更清晰的分析路径，通过SORT问题格式、分层估计程序和LLM辅助匹配，有效区分了暴露量与危害率趋势，并为治理决策提供了有意义的轨迹分类。

Abstract: Public AI incident database counts conflate changes in reporting propensity, deployment growth, and shifts in harm frequency per unit of exposure. These issues introduce significant uncertainties challenging public and corporate policy frameworks centred on realized risks. We propose a simple framework that establishes clear points of inquiry, separately estimates exposure from harm-rate trends, and then classifies into meaningful trajectory categories for governance decisions. The framework combines a structured monitoring question format (SORT) to clarify coverage decisions, a tiered estimation procedure calibrated to available evidence, and LLM-assisted incident matching against public databases. Applied to various monitoring questions, we draw conclusions regarding the monitoring ecosystem more broadly: Providing an essential interpretative classification, determining what can and cannot be claimed, and establishing that exposure estimation is required as AI deployments become increasingly common.

</details>


### [136] [Context-Aware Displacement Estimation from Mobile Phone Data: A Methodological Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21457)
*Rajius Idzalika,Muhammad Rheza Muztahid,Radityo Eko Prasojo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于手机数据的实时人口位移检测方法框架，通过区分居民与通勤者类型、考虑星期和用户类型的上下文感知检测、以及基于基线变异系数的操作不确定性边界，解决现有方法将常规通勤者误分类为流离失所者的问题。在菲律宾阿帕里市超级台风南多后的案例验证中，上下文感知检测在工作日将市际位移估计减少1.6-2.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人口位移评估依赖现场调查，速度慢且无法实时响应；现有手机数据方法采用统一位移定义，忽视了个体出行模式差异，导致将规律性跨市通勤者错误识别为灾害相关流离失所者，影响人道主义决策的准确性。

Method: 框架包含三大创新：(1) 流动性画像分类——区分本地居民与通勤者类型；(2) 上下文感知的市际位移检测——根据用户类型和星期几计算预期位置；(3) 操作不确定性边界——基于基线变异系数与灾害调整因子，用于人道主义决策支持而非正式统计推断。产出三类指标：位移率、起点-终点流量、返乡动态。

Result: 在菲律宾阿帕里市（2026年超级台风南多后）基于Globe Telecom vendor提供的每日位置数据验证：上下文感知检测在工作日相比朴素方法减少市际位移估计1.6-2.7个百分点，主要归因于"通勤者豁免"，但该结果未经独立验证。

Conclusion: 框架仅覆盖市际位移，市内疏散不在范围内。单案例演示确立概念验证，外部有效性需多事件多地点应用。方法通过聚合保护隐私，为人道主义行动提供可操作的位移信息。

Abstract: Timely population displacement estimates are critical for humanitarian response during disasters, but traditional surveys and field assessments are slow. Mobile phone data enables near real-time tracking, yet existing approaches apply uniform displacement definitions regardless of individual mobility patterns, misclassifying regular commuters as displaced. We present a methodological framework addressing this through three innovations: (1) mobility profile classification distinguishing local residents from commuter types, (2) context-aware between-municipality displacement detection accounting for expected location by user type and day of week, and (3) operational uncertainty bounds derived from baseline coefficient of variation with a disaster adjustment factor, intended for humanitarian decision support rather than formal statistical inference. The framework produces three complementary metrics scaled to population with uncertainty bounds: displacement rates, origin-destination flows, and return dynamics. An Aparri case study following Super Typhoon Nando (2025, Philippines) applies the framework to vendor-provided daily locations from Globe Telecom. Context-aware detection reduced estimated between-municipality displacement by 1.6-2.7 percentage points on weekdays versus naive methods, attributable to the commuter exception but not independently validated. The method captures between-municipality displacement only. Within-municipality evacuation falls outside scope. The single-case demonstration establishes proof of concept. External validity requires application across multiple events and locations. The framework provides humanitarian actors with operational displacement information while preserving individual privacy through aggregation.

</details>


### [137] [FAccT-Checked: A Narrative Review of Authority Reconfigurations and Retention in AI-Mediated Journalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21864)
*Stefano Sorrentino,Matilde Barbini,Daniel Gatica-Perez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper develops a theoretical framework analyzing how AI adoption drives dual authority migrations in journalism: internally via LLM integration in newsrooms, and externally via platform/vendor consolidation. Both processes undermine editorial accountability and transparency, and while participatory AI design offers potential remedies, its effectiveness is structurally constrained.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The proliferation of AI in newsrooms raises fundamental questions about how editorial authority is transformed, who holds it, and how fairness, accountability, and transparency concerns emerge and persist when algorithmic systems mediate journalistic practice.

Method: The study employs a critical narrative review methodology, integrating scholarship across journalism studies, human-computer interaction (HCI), and FAccT (Fairness, Accountability, and Transparency). Editorial authority is conceptualized as a three-dimensional construct: decision rights, epistemic warrant, and responsibility.

Result: The paper identifies two concurrent authority reconfigurations: (1) internal migration—LLMs embedded in newsroom workflows progressively defer editorial judgment through interactional, cognitive, and organizational mechanisms that obscure responsibility; (2) external migration—power shifts from news organizations to platforms, vendors, and infrastructure providers, deepening existing power asymmetries. These processes risk rendering fairness unsustainable, accountability unassignable, and transparency merely performative.

Conclusion: The paper concludes by critically examining participatory AI design approaches as potential remedies, while exposing their structural limitations—participation may either meaningfully redistribute authority or operate as tokenism that preserves existing power asymmetries.

Abstract: Building on recent interpretivist approaches, we conduct a critical narrative review across journalism studies, human-computer interaction, and FAccT scholarship, conceptualizing editorial authority as the conjunction of decision rights, epistemic warrant, and responsibility. We provide a comprehensive theoretical framework for addressing how concerns on fairness, accountability and transparency emerge, interact, and persist within AI mediated journalistic practice. We identify and describe two concurrent authority reconfigurations driven by AI adoption. First, an internal migration of authority, in which editorial judgment is progressively deferred to large language models (LLMs) embedded within newsroom workflows. This migration occurs not through explicit policy decisions, but through interactional, cognitive, and organizational mechanisms that legitimize AI generated outputs while obscuring responsibility and weakening individual and professional agency. Second, we analyze an external migration of authority, whereby decision making power shifts from news organizations toward platforms, vendors, and infrastructural providers that supply AI systems and distribution channels, exacerbating existing power asymmetries within the media ecosystem. Unaddressed, these reconfigurations risk rendering fairness hard to maintain, accountability difficult to assign and transparency performative. We examine participatory approaches to AI design and deployment in journalism as potential mechanisms for retaining or reclaiming editorial authority. We critically assess both their promise and their structural limitations, highlighting how participation can either meaningfully redistribute authority or function as a tokenistic practice that leaves underlying power relations intact.

</details>


### [138] [Equity Bias: An Ethical Framework for AI Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21907)
*Mary Lockwood*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出Equity Bias框架，一种基于诠释论哲学和认知不正义理论的AI公平性方法。该框架突破传统偏见消除思维，转而追求使偏见透明化和可争议化。通过三阶段生命周期方法论（公平考古学、共同创造意义、持续问责），引导开发者、研究者和政策制定者构建更具伦理问责能力、更能应对复杂现实挑战的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI偏见治理方法将偏见视为需消除的错误，但忽视了偏见背后反映的是谁的知识被编码进系统。这一路径无法从根本上解决AI系统中的知识代表性问题，也无法触及偏见的社会认识论根源。

Method: 提出三阶段AI生命周期方法论：①Equity Archaeology（公平考古学）：映射知识与假设；②Co-Creating Meaning（共同创造意义）：参与式设计；③Ongoing Accountability（持续问责）：持续评估与监督。

Result: Equity Bias框架实现了三个核心目标：使偏见变得透明和可质疑；拓宽AI系统视角的塑造者范围；提供将AI系统理解为诠释性智能主体的新视角。

Conclusion: Equity Bias框架为开发者、研究者和政策制定者提供了系统性指南，推动AI系统的伦理问责制，并使其具备应对复杂现实挑战的能力。该框架将AI系统重新定位为具有诠释能力的智能主体，而非中立的工具。

Abstract: Equity Bias is a philosophical and practical framework for building smarter, more equitable AI systems. Grounded in hermeneutic philosophy and epistemic injustice theory, it treats bias not as an error to eliminate but as a reflection of whose knowledge is encoded into systems. While traditional approaches aim to reduce or remove bias, Equity Bias instead makes bias transparent and contestable. In doing so, it broadens whose perspectives shape AI and provides a lens for understanding AI systems as interpretive agents. The framework introduces a three-phase AI Life Cycle methodology: 'Equity Archaeology' (mapping knowledge and assumptions), 'Co-Creating Meaning' (participatory design), and 'Ongoing Accountability' (continuous evaluation). Equity Bias guides developers, researchers, and policymakers towards AI that is ethically accountable and capable of addressing complex real-world challenges.

</details>

### [139] [Architectures for Robust Self-Organizing Energy Systems under Information and Control Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21529)
*Emilie Frost,Astrid Nieße*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Applying the concept of controlled self-organization in agent-based Cyber-Physical Energy Systems (CPES) is a promising approach to ensure system robustness. By introducing an observer/controller architecture to the system, this concept allows for self-organization while still enabling intervention when disturbances occur. Thus, it is possible to respond to effects of cyber attacks, a major threat to current energy systems. However, when implementing an observer to monitor the system and a controller to execute actions for controlled self-organization in CPES, it is essential to take into account restrictions on information and actions resulting from the privacy of local distributed energy resources, regulatory constraints, and data exchange requirements. For this reason, this paper presents architecture variants for the observer and controller that take into account restrictions on access to information and limited actions. In addition, it evaluates possible controller actions in various architectures. The results underscore the importance of considering observer/controller architectures when designing agent-based systems to ensure their robustness for real-world applications.

</details>
