Extensive experiments across multiple gender bias evaluation settings demonstrate that FairQE consistently improves gender fairness over strong QE baselines. Moreover, under MQM-based meta-evaluation following the WMT 2023 Metrics Shared Task, FairQE achieves competitive or improved general QE performance. These results show that gender bias in QE can be effectively mitigated without sacrificing evaluation accuracy, enabling fairer and more reliable translation evaluation.

</details>


### [179] [Brief chatbot interactions produce lasting changes in human moral values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21430)
*Yue Teng,Qianer Zhong,Kim Mai Tich Nguyen Thordsen,Christian Montag,Benjamin Becker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究探讨了AI聊天机器人是否会影响人类的道德判断。结果表明，简短的对话即可显著改变道德评价，且这种影响具有持久性，同时参与者并未意识到这种说服意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能聊天机器人越来越多地作为个人顾问角色出现，但它们对道德判断的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 采用被试内自然主义范式，53名参与者首先对道德情境进行评分，然后与被设定为引导道德判断转向的聊天机器人讨论四个情境，并与控制Agent讨论另外四个情境。

Result: 简短的对话诱导了道德判断的显著方向性转变，包括接受更严格标准和倡导更大宽容两种情况（p < 0.05，Cohen's d = 0.735-1.576）。两周随访时效果增强（Cohen's d = 1.038-2.069）。控制条件未产生变化，效果未扩展至惩罚判断。参与者未察觉说服意图，两个Agent在受欢迎程度和说服力评分上无差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人类对AI聊天机器人 undetected（未被察觉）和 lasting（持久性）操纵基础道德价值观的脆弱性。

Abstract: Moral judgements form the foundation of human social behavior and societal systems. While Artificial Intelligence chatbots increasingly serve as personal advisors, their influence on moral judgments remains largely unexplored. Here, we examined whether directive AI conversations shift moral evaluations using a within-subject naturalistic paradigm. Fifty-three participants rated moral scenarios, then discussed four with a chatbot prompted to shift moral judgments and four with a control agent. The brief conversations induced significant directional shifts in moral judgments, accepting stricter standards as well as advocating greater leniency (ps < 0.05; Cohen's d = 0.735-1.576), with increasing strengths of this effect during a two-week follow-up (Cohen's d = 1.038-2.069). Critically, the control condition produced no changes, and the effects did not extend to punishment while participants remained unaware of the persuasive intent, and both agents were rated equally likable and convincing, suggesting a vulnerability to undetected and lasting manipulation of foundational moral values.

</details>


### [180] [HiCrew: Hierarchical Reasoning for Long-Form Video Understanding via Question-Aware Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21444)
*Yuehan Zhu,Jingqi Zhao,Jiawen Zhao,Xudong Mao,Baoquan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HiCrew层级多智能体框架，通过Hybrid Tree结构保持时间拓扑、Question-Aware Captioning生成精准描述、Planning Layer动态编排智能体协作三大创新，解决长视频理解中的时空冗余和叙事依赖问题，在EgoSchema和NExT-QA数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解面临时空冗余和长时叙事依赖的双重挑战；现有结构化表示虽能压缩视觉信息但牺牲了时间连贯性；现有多智能体框架采用刚性预定义工作流，无法根据问题特定需求自适应调整推理策略。

Method: 提出三大核心贡献：1）Hybrid Tree结构：利用镜头边界检测保持时间拓扑，同时在语义一致片段内进行相关性引导的层级聚类；2）Question-Aware Captioning机制：合成意图驱动的视觉提示以生成精确导向的语义描述；3）Planning Layer：基于问题复杂度动态编排智能体协作，自适应选择角色和执行路径。

Result: 在EgoSchema和NExT-QA数据集上验证有效，在多种问题类型上表现强劲，尤其在时间和因果推理任务上提升显著。

Conclusion: 实验验证表明该框架在多种问题类型上均表现优异，尤其在时间和因果推理任务上优势显著，验证了层级结构保持设计的有效性。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding remains fundamentally challenged by pervasive spatiotemporal redundancy and intricate narrative dependencies that span extended temporal horizons. While recent structured representations compress visual information effectively, they frequently sacrifice temporal coherence, which is critical for causal reasoning. Meanwhile, existing multi-agent frameworks operate through rigid, pre-defined workflows that fail to adapt their reasoning strategies to question-specific demands. In this paper, we introduce HiCrew, a hierarchical multi-agent framework that addresses these limitations through three core contributions. First, we propose a Hybrid Tree structure that leverages shot boundary detection to preserve temporal topology while performing relevance-guided hierarchical clustering within semantically coherent segments. Second, we develop a Question-Aware Captioning mechanism that synthesizes intent-driven visual prompts to generate precision-oriented semantic descriptions. Third, we integrate a Planning Layer that dynamically orchestrates agent collaboration by adaptively selecting roles and execution paths based on question complexity. Extensive experiments on EgoSchema and NExT-QA validate the effectiveness of our approach, demonstrating strong performance across diverse question types with particularly pronounced gains in temporal and causal reasoning tasks that benefit from our hierarchical structure-preserving design.

</details>


### [181] [GeoMind: An Agentic Workflow for Lithology Classification with Reasoned Tool Invocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21501)
*Yitong Zhou,Mingyue Cheng,Jiahao Wang,Qingyang Mao,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoMind，一种将岩性分类建模为顺序推理过程的工具增强智能体框架。通过感知、推理、分析三模块协同工作，全局规划器自适应协调，基于细粒度过程监督确保决策轨迹有效性和地质约束一致性。实验证明该方法在分类性能和可解释性方面均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将岩性分类问题形式化为静态单步判别映射，缺少领域先验知识，导致预测结果与地质实际脱节，无法进行基于证据的符合地质标准的诊断推理。

Method: (1) 构建了三模块工具包：感知模块将原始测井数据转化为语义趋势，推理模块从多源证据推断岩性假设，分析模块验证预测与地层约束的一致性；(2) 设计全局规划器自适应协调各模块；(3) 提出细粒度过程监督策略，优化中间推理步骤而非仅关注最终结果。

Result: GeoMind在四个基准测井数据集上持续优于强基线方法，既提升了分类性能，又提供了透明可追溯的决策过程。

Conclusion: GeoMind通过工具增强的智能体框架，结合全局规划器和过程监督机制，在四个基准数据集上实现了优于强基线的分类性能，同时提供了透明可追溯的决策过程。

Abstract: Lithology classification in well logs is a fundamental geoscience data mining task that aims to infer rock types from multi dimensional geophysical sequences. Despite recent progress, existing approaches typically formulate the problem as a static, single-step discriminative mapping. This static paradigm limits evidence-based diagnostic reasoning against geological standards, often yielding predictions that are detached from geological reality due to a lack of domain priors. In this work, we propose GeoMind, a tool-augmented agentic framework that models lithology classification as a sequential reasoning process. GeoMind organizes its toolkit into perception, reasoning, and analysis modules, which respectively translate raw logs into semantic trends, infer lithology hypotheses from multi-source evidence, and verify predictions against stratigraphic constraints. A global planner adaptively coordinates these modules based on input characteristics, enabling geologically plausible and evidence-grounded decisions. To guarantee the logical consistency of GeoMind, we introduce a fine-grained process supervision strategy. Unlike standard methods that focus solely on final outcomes, our approach optimizes intermediate reasoning steps, ensuring the validity of decision trajectories and alignment to geological constraints. Experiments on four benchmark well-log datasets demonstrate that GeoMind consistently outperforms strong baselines in classification performance while providing transparent and traceable decision-making processes.

</details>


### [182] [BioMiner: A Multi-modal System for Automated Mining of Protein-Ligand Bioactivity Data from Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21508)
*Jiaxian Yan,Jintao Zhu,Yuhang Yang,Qi Liu,Kai Zhang,Zaixi Zhang,Xukai Liu,Boyan Zhang,Kaiyuan Gao,Jinchuan Xiao,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioMiner是一个多模态生物活性数据提取框架，通过将语义解释与配体结构构建分离来从科学文献中提取蛋白质-配体生物活性数据。在包含16,457条条目的BioVista基准测试上达到0.32的F1分数，并通过三个应用场景证明了其实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质-配体生物活性数据对手动整理的速度难以跟上文献增长，人工智能辅助提取面临挑战：需要解释分布文本、表格和图表中的生化语义，并重建化学精确的配体结构（如Markush结构）。

Method: BioMiner采用多模态提取框架，将生物活性语义解释与配体结构构建显式分离。语义推理通过直接推理完成，化学结构解析采用化学结构引导的视觉语义推理范式——多模态大语言模型在化学基础视觉表示上推理结构关系，精确分子构建由领域化学工具完成。

Result: 建立BioVista基准测试（含16,457条生物活性条目，来自500篇文献）；BioMiner在生物活性三元组提取上达到0.32的F1分数。

Conclusion: 三个应用验证其实用性：(1) 从11,683篇论文提取82,262条数据构建预训练数据库，下游模型性能提升3.9%；(2) 人机协同工作流使NLRP3生物活性数据量翻倍，28个QSAR模型提升38.6%，并识别16个新骨架hit候选物；(3) 蛋白质-配体复合物生物活性注释加速5.59倍，PoseBusters数据集准确率提升5.75%。

Abstract: Protein-ligand bioactivity data published in the literature are essential for drug discovery, yet manual curation struggles to keep pace with rapidly growing literature. Automated bioactivity extraction remains challenging because it requires not only interpreting biochemical semantics distributed across text, tables, and figures, but also reconstructing chemically exact ligand structures (e.g., Markush structures). To address this bottleneck, we introduce BioMiner, a multi-modal extraction framework that explicitly separates bioactivity semantic interpretation from ligand structure construction. Within BioMiner, bioactivity semantics are inferred through direct reasoning, while chemical structures are resolved via a chemical-structure-grounded visual semantic reasoning paradigm, in which multi-modal large language models operate on chemically grounded visual representations to infer inter-structure relationships, and exact molecular construction is delegated to domain chemistry tools. For rigorous evaluation and method development, we further establish BioVista, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 16,457 bioactivity entries curated from 500 publications. BioMiner validates its extraction ability and provides a quantitative baseline, achieving an F1 score of 0.32 for bioactivity triplets. BioMiner's practical utility is demonstrated via three applications: (1) extracting 82,262 data from 11,683 papers to build a pre-training database that improves downstream models performance by 3.9%; (2) enabling a human-in-the-loop workflow that doubles the number of high-quality NLRP3 bioactivity data, helping 38.6% improvement over 28 QSAR models and identification of 16 hit candidates with novel scaffolds; and (3) accelerating protein-ligand complex bioactivity annotation, achieving a 5.59-fold speed increase and 5.75% accuracy improvement over manual workflows in PoseBusters dataset.

</details>


### [183] [Unbiased Prevalence Estimation with Multicalibrated LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21549)
*Fridolin Linder,Thomas Leeper,Daniel Haimovich,Niek Tax,Lorenzo Perini,Milan Vojnovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对使用不完美测量设备进行人群类别流行率估计时标准方法在协变量偏移下产生偏差的问题，提出多校准方法可保证无偏估计，并通过对就业调查和政治文本分类的实证验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用不完美测量设备（诊断测试、分类器或大语言模型）估计人群中某类别的流行率是科学、公共卫生和在线信任安全的基础性问题。传统方法假设设备错误率在不同人群间保持稳定，但研究表明这一假设在协变量偏移条件下不成立。

Method: 采用多校准（multicalibration）方法，即在输入特征条件而非仅在平均意义上强制执行校准。研究将近期公平性理论工作与长期测量问题相联系，并通过模拟实验验证，同时在两个实证场景中应用：使用美国社区调查估计各州就业流行率，以及使用大语言模型对四个国家的政治文本进行分类。

Result: 模拟实验证实标准方法偏差随偏移量增加而增长，而多校准估计量保持接近零的偏差。两个实证应用表明多校准在实际中显著减少偏差。研究同时揭示校准数据应覆盖目标人群可能差异的关键特征维度。

Conclusion: 理论结果可应用于任何分类模型。实证表明多校准能显著减少估计偏差，但实践中需要确保校准数据覆盖目标人群可能存在差异的关键特征维度。多校准方法弥合了近期公平性理论与横跨几乎所有学科的长期测量问题之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Estimating the prevalence of a category in a population using imperfect measurement devices (diagnostic tests, classifiers, or large language models) is fundamental to science, public health, and online trust and safety. Standard approaches correct for known device error rates but assume these rates remain stable across populations. We show this assumption fails under covariate shift and that multicalibration, which enforces calibration conditional on the input features rather than just on average, is sufficient for unbiased prevalence estimation under such shift. Standard calibration and quantification methods fail to provide this guarantee. Our work connects recent theoretical work on fairness to a longstanding measurement problem spanning nearly all academic disciplines. A simulation confirms that standard methods exhibit bias growing with shift magnitude, while a multicalibrated estimator maintains near-zero bias. While we focus the discussion mostly on LLMs, our theoretical results apply to any classification model. Two empirical applications -- estimating employment prevalence across U.S. states using the American Community Survey, and classifying political texts across four countries using an LLM -- demonstrate that multicalibration substantially reduces bias in practice, while highlighting that calibration data should cover the key feature dimensions along which target populations may differ.

</details>


### [184] [Engaged AI Governance: Addressing the Last Mile Challenge Through Internal Expert Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21554)
*Simon Jarvers,Orestis Papakyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Under the EU AI Act, translating AI governance requirements into software development practice remains challenging. While AI governance frameworks exist at industry and organizational levels, empirical evidence of team-level implementation is scarce. We address this "Last Mile" Challenge through insider action research embedded within an AI startup. We present a legal-text-to-action pipeline that translates EU AI Act requirements into actionable strategies through internal expert collaboration by extracting requirements from legal text, engaging practitioners in assessment and ideation, and prioritizing implementation through collective evaluation. Our analysis reveals three patterns in how practitioners perceive regulatory requirements: convergence (compliance aligns with development priorities), existing practice (current work already satisfies requirements), and disconnection (requirements perceived as administrative overhead). Based on these patterns, we discuss when governance might be treated genuinely or performatively. Practitioners prioritize requirements that serve end-users or their own development needs, but view verification-oriented requirements as box-ticking exercises. This distinction suggests a translation challenge: regulatory requirements risk superficial treatment unless practitioners understand how compliance serves system quality and user protection. Expert collaboration offers a practical mechanism for transforming governance from external imposition to shared ownership and making previously invisible governance work visible and collective.

</details>


### [185] [To See the Unseen: on the Generalization Ability of Transformers in Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21632)
*Nevena Lazić,Liam Fowl,András György,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Decoder-only transformer模型在符号推理任务中无法泛化到未见token的根本原因不仅是生成困难，还包括(un)embedding坍缩导致未见token无法被区分。通过架构改进、数据多样化和embedding重置等组合技术可解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明decoder-only transformer模型在遇到训练时未观察到的变量名时无法泛化，一个关键原因是生成未见 token 的困难。该研究旨在深入理解这一泛化失败的机制。

Method: 通过受控实验验证理论预测，并探索了促进 copying 的架构改进、数据多样化和(un)embedding冻结/重置等技术的组合效果。

Result: 理论分析和实验表明，(un)embedding坍缩是导致泛化失败的另一个关键原因：训练时未见 token 的最后一层权重收敛到几乎相同的向量，使得模型难以区分多个未见变量。通过组合技术（架构改变+数据增强+embedding重置）可以解决此问题。

Conclusion: 基于这一发现，研究者通过架构改进、数据多样化和embedding冻结/重置等组合技术实现了对未见 token 的泛化。实验还发现 Gemma 3 系列模型中的99个未使用 token 确实存在(un)embedding坍缩问题，这些相关 embedding 对微调应用是不良初始化。

Abstract: We investigate the ability of decoder-only transformer models to perform abstract symbolic reasoning; specifically solving propositional logic reasoning problems given in-context. Previous work demonstrated that models fail to generalize to problems involving variable names that were not observed during training, and it was shown that one reason behind this is the difficulty of copying (or generating) unseen tokens. We show both theoretically and empirically that a particular representational collapse also has a crucial role: the unembeddings (last-layer weights) of unseen tokens collapse to nearly the same vector during training. The collapse makes distinguishing multiple unseen variables difficult for the model (especially when the embedding and unembedding parameters are shared), and provides a mechanistic explanation for the effectiveness of existing heuristic interventions like "active forgetting", which periodically reset the token (un)embeddings. Based on these observations, we devise a combination of techniques, involving a small architecture change facilitating copying, data diversity, and freezing or resetting (un)embeddings, that achieves generalization to unseen tokens. We support our claims with extensive controlled experiments on propositional logic reasoning problems. Beyond synthetic experiments, we also observe evidence of (un)embedding collapse in the open-weight models in the Gemma 3 family, which includes 99 unused tokens reserved for downstream use. Empirically we find that the correlated embeddings of these tokens are a poor initialization for finetuning applications.

</details>


### [186] [GS-Quant: Granular Semantic and Generative Structural Quantization for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21649)
*Qizhuo Xie,Yunhui Liu,Yu Xing,Qianzi Hou,Xudong Jin,Tao Zheng,Tieke He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GS-Quant框架，通过语义增强和结构重建模块，将知识图谱嵌入转化为层次化离散代码，解决LLM与图嵌入的模态差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法将量化视为平坦数值压缩，代码语义纠缠，无法反映人类推理的层次性逻辑。

Method: 引入Granular Semantic Enhancement模块注入层次知识，Generative Structural Reconstruction模块施加因果依赖，将代码序列转化为结构化语义描述符。

Result: 显著优于基于文本和基于嵌入的基线方法，实现了图结构推理与自然语言生成的结构同构。

Conclusion: GS-Quant成功生成语义连贯且结构分层的离散代码，为LLM与知识图谱的深度融合提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown immense potential in Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC), yet bridging the modality gap between continuous graph embeddings and discrete LLM tokens remains a critical challenge. While recent quantization-based approaches attempt to align these modalities, they typically treat quantization as flat numerical compression, resulting in semantically entangled codes that fail to mirror the hierarchical nature of human reasoning. In this paper, we propose GS-Quant, a novel framework that generates semantically coherent and structurally stratified discrete codes for KG entities. Unlike prior methods, GS-Quant is grounded in the insight that entity representations should follow a linguistic coarse-to-fine logic. We introduce a Granular Semantic Enhancement module that injects hierarchical knowledge into the codebook, ensuring that earlier codes capture global semantic categories while later codes refine specific attributes. Furthermore, a Generative Structural Reconstruction module imposes causal dependencies on the code sequence, transforming independent discrete units into structured semantic descriptors. By expanding the LLM vocabulary with these learned codes, we enable the model to reason over graph structures isomorphically to natural language generation. Experimental results demonstrate that GS-Quant significantly outperforms existing text-based and embedding-based baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/mikumifa/GS-Quant.

</details>


### [187] [Enabling and Inhibitory Pathways of University Students' Willingness to Disclose AI Use: A Cognition-Affect-Conation Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21733)
*Yiran Du,Huimin He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于认知-情感-意动(CAC)框架，采用混合方法研究设计，探讨大学生披露AI辅助作业的心理机制。研究发现心理安全感促进披露意愿，而评估焦虑抑制披露意愿，两者在制度支持与政策清晰度的影响下相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能在高等教育中的整合日益增加，学生在报告AI辅助作业时的透明度问题变得尤为重要。现有研究对驱动学生披露AI使用意愿的心理机制缺乏深入理解。

Method: 采用顺序解释性混合方法设计。定量阶段：收集546名大学生问卷数据，使用结构方程模型分析认知感知、情感反应与披露意愿之间的关系。定性阶段：对22名学生进行半结构化访谈，深入解释定量发现。

Result: 心理安全感显著提高学生披露AI使用的意愿，并受到感知公平、感知教师支持和感知组织支持的正向影响。评估焦虑降低披露意愿和心理安全感，且被感知污名、感知不确定性和隐私顾虑所强化。定性发现进一步揭示：制度清晰和支持性教学实践鼓励开放态度，而政策模糊和对负面评价的恐惧导致学生采取谨慎或策略性披露行为。

Conclusion: 研究强调了促进AI使用披露的赋能机制与抑制机制的二重性，凸显了支持性制度环境和明确指导对促进高等教育中负责任AI透明度的重要性。

Abstract: The increasing integration of artificial intelligence (AI) in higher education has raised important questions regarding students' transparency in reporting AI-assisted work. This study investigates the psychological mechanisms underlying university students' willingness to disclose AI use by applying the Cognition--Affect--Conation (CAC) framework. A sequential explanatory mixed-methods design was employed. In the quantitative phase, survey data were collected from 546 university students and analysed using structural equation modelling to examine the relationships among cognitive perceptions, affective responses, and disclosure intention. In the qualitative phase, semi-structured interviews with 22 students were conducted to further interpret the quantitative findings. The results indicate that psychological safety significantly increases students' willingness to disclose AI use and is positively shaped by perceived fairness, perceived teacher support, and perceived organisational support. Conversely, evaluation apprehension reduces disclosure intention and psychological safety, and is strengthened by perceived stigma, perceived uncertainty, and privacy concern. Qualitative findings further reveal that institutional clarity and supportive instructional practices encourage openness, whereas policy ambiguity and fear of negative evaluation often lead students to adopt cautious or strategic disclosure practices. Overall, the study highlights the dual role of enabling and inhibitory psychological mechanisms in shaping AI-use disclosure and underscores the importance of supportive institutional environments and clear guidance for promoting responsible AI transparency in higher education.

</details>


### [188] [Thinking with Reasoning Skills: Fewer Tokens, More Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21764)
*Guangxiang Zhao,Qilong Shi,Xusen Xiao,Xiangzheng Zhang,Tong Yang,Lin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reasoning LLMs often spend substantial tokens on long intermediate reasoning traces (e.g., chain-of-thought) when solving new problems. We propose to summarize and store reusable reasoning skills distilled from extensive deliberation and trial-and-error exploration, and to retrieve these skills at inference time to guide future reasoning. Unlike the prevailing \emph{reasoning from scratch} paradigm, our approach first recalls relevant skills for each query, helping the model avoid redundant detours and focus on effective solution paths. We evaluate our method on coding and mathematical reasoning tasks, and find that it significantly reduces reasoning tokens while improving overall performance. The resulting lower per-request cost indicates strong practical and economic potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [189] [Inferring High-Level Events from Timestamped Data: Complexity and Medical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21793)
*Yvon K. Awuklu,Meghyn Bienvenu,Katsumi Inoue,Vianney Jouhet,Fleur Mougin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逻辑的方法，从带时间戳的数据和背景知识中检测高级时间扩展事件。通过逻辑规则捕获简单事件的存亡条件并组合为元事件，使用约束识别不兼容事件组合，并通过修复机制选择一致事件集。系统在肺癌用例上验证了计算可行性和与医学专家的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要从带时间戳的数据中检测高级时间扩展事件；在医学领域，需要从临床观察（如诊断和用药记录）中推断疾病事件和治疗过程；推断可能产生错误事件，需要处理。

Method: 采用逻辑规则捕获简单时间事件的存在和终止条件，并将其组合为元事件；使用约束识别不兼容的事件组合；提出修复机制选择优选的一致事件集；通过回答集编程(ASP)实现核心组件；识别确保多项式时间数据复杂度的相关限制。

Result: 原型系统在肺癌用例上的评估表明计算可行，且结果与医学专家意见高度一致。

Conclusion: 框架是通用的，可复用于医疗领域以外的其他领域。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a novel logic-based approach to detecting high-level temporally extended events from timestamped data and background knowledge. Our framework employs logical rules to capture existence and termination conditions for simple temporal events and to combine these into meta-events. In the medical domain, for example, disease episodes and therapies are inferred from timestamped clinical observations, such as diagnoses and drug administrations stored in patient records, and can be further combined into higher-level disease events. As some incorrect events might be inferred, we use constraints to identify incompatible combinations of events and propose a repair mechanism to select preferred consistent sets of events. While reasoning in the full framework is intractable, we identify relevant restrictions that ensure polynomial-time data complexity. Our prototype system implements core components of the approach using answer set programming. An evaluation on a lung cancer use case supports the interest of the approach, both in terms of computational feasibility and positive alignment of our results with medical expert opinions. While strongly motivated by the needs of the healthcare domain, our framework is purposely generic, enabling its reuse in other areas.

</details>


### [190] [Alignment has a Fantasia Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21827)
*Nathanael Jo,Zoe De Simone,Mitchell Gordon,Ashia Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Fantasia交互"概念，指AI系统将用户提示视为完整意图表达而忽视用户目标尚未完全形成的现象，并主张AI应通过主动帮助用户形成和细化意图来提供认知支持，而非仅被动响应指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐研究隐含假设用户能清晰表达目标和需求，但行为科学研究表明人们在接触AI系统时往往目标尚未完全明确。当AI将提示视为完整意图表达时，虽看似有用便捷，却未必真正契合用户需求，导致对齐失败。

Method: 跨学科综合研究，整合机器学习、界面设计、行为科学三领域视角，剖析Fantasia交互的机制与失败模式，评估现有干预措施的不足，并提出新的研究议程。

Result: 论文识别了Fantasia交互的核心问题：AI系统设计前提与人类实际认知过程的根本矛盾。指出当前干预措施的局限性，并论证了需要从"响应式工具"向"认知支持伙伴"转变的研究方向。

Conclusion: Fantasia交互问题要求重新思考对齐研究范式：AI系统应具备主动帮助用户探索和明确任务不确定性的能力。这需要建立新的设计原则和评估框架，以更好地支持人类在目标形成过程中的认知需求。

Abstract: Modern AI assistants are trained to follow instructions, implicitly assuming that users can clearly articulate their goals and the kind of assistance they need. Decades of behavioral research, however, show that people often engage with AI systems before their goals are fully formed. When AI systems treat prompts as complete expressions of intent, they can appear to be useful or convenient, but not necessarily aligned with the users' needs. We call these failures Fantasia interactions. We argue that Fantasia interactions demand a rethinking of alignment research: rather than treating users as rational oracles, AI should provide cognitive support by actively helping users form and refine their intent through time. This requires an interdisciplinary approach that bridges machine learning, interface design, and behavioral science. We synthesize insights from these fields to characterize the mechanisms and failures of Fantasia interactions. We then show why existing interventions are insufficient, and propose a research agenda for designing and evaluating AI systems that better help humans navigate uncertainty in their tasks.

</details>


### [191] [Nemobot Games: Crafting Strategic AI Gaming Agents for Interactive Learning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21896)
*Chee Wei Tan,Yuchen Wang,Shangxin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLMs）扩展香农游戏机器分类法的新范式，并开发了名为Nemobot的交互式智能体工程环境。该系统通过LLM驱动的聊天机器人在四类游戏中展示能力：词典游戏、严格可解游戏、启发式游戏和学习型游戏，并提供工具增强生成和策略智能体微调功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展并操作化香农（Claude Shannon）的游戏机器分类法，利用LLM实现更高级的游戏智能体编程能力

Method: Nemobot环境集成LLM聊天机器人，通过四种策略框架处理不同游戏类别：(1)词典游戏：将状态-动作映射压缩为高效泛化模型；(2)严格可解游戏：运用数学推理计算最优策略并生成可解释决策；(3)启发式游戏：结合经典minimax算法与众包数据综合策略；(4)学习型游戏：利用人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）和自我批评通过试错和模仿学习迭代优化策略

Result: Nemobot成功展示了在战略游戏到角色扮演游戏中的多游戏类型适应能力，实现了工具增强生成和战略游戏智能体微调功能

Conclusion: 通过整合众包学习和人类创造力迭代优化自身逻辑，展示了AI智能体的自我编程潜能，是迈向自我编程AI长期目标的重要一步

Abstract: This paper introduces a new paradigm for AI game programming, leveraging large language models (LLMs) to extend and operationalize Claude Shannon's taxonomy of game-playing machines. Central to this paradigm is Nemobot, an interactive agentic engineering environment that enables users to create, customize, and deploy LLM-powered game agents while actively engaging with AI-driven strategies. The LLM-based chatbot, integrated within Nemobot, demonstrates its capabilities across four distinct classes of games. For dictionary-based games, it compresses state-action mappings into efficient, generalized models for rapid adaptability. In rigorously solvable games, it employs mathematical reasoning to compute optimal strategies and generates human-readable explanations for its decisions. For heuristic-based games, it synthesizes strategies by combining insights from classical minimax algorithms (see, e.g., shannon1950chess) with crowd-sourced data. Finally, in learning-based games, it utilizes reinforcement learning with human feedback and self-critique to iteratively refine strategies through trial-and-error and imitation learning. Nemobot amplifies this framework by offering a programmable environment where users can experiment with tool-augmented generation and fine-tuning of strategic game agents. From strategic games to role-playing games, Nemobot demonstrates how AI agents can achieve a form of self-programming by integrating crowdsourced learning and human creativity to iteratively refine their own logic. This represents a step toward the long-term goal of self-programming AI.

</details>


### [192] [Handbook of Rough Set Extensions and Uncertainty Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19794)
*Takaaki Fujita,Florentin Smarandache*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本书以"模型地图"为定位，系统梳理粗糙集理论的主要范式及其扩展路径，而非聚焦于特征约简与规则归纳等传统主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙集理论通过由不可分辨关系或数据表中的粒化关系诱导的下近似集和上近似集来建模不确定性，捕捉由观测分辨率受限导致的模糊性，支持关于确定性知识与仅可能性知识的集合论推理。

Method: 本书按两个维度组织代表性变体：(i) 粒化机制底层类型，包括等价类型、容差型、覆盖型、邻域型和概率型近似；(ii) 数据与关系的不确定性语义，包括清晰、模糊、直觉模糊、中智和叠融设置。采用小规模illustrative examples辅助说明建模意图与典型应用场景。

Result: 阐明不同粒化机制与不确定性语义选择如何改变近似集的数学形式及边界区域的解释方式，为分类与决策支持提供建模指导。

Conclusion: 本书的核心贡献在于为粗糙集模型及其扩展提供一个系统化、结构化的研究框架与学术导航，而非开发单一算法管道。特征约简与规则诱导仅作为动机性应用和学术入口被简要提及。

Abstract: Rough set theory models uncertainty by approximating target concepts through lower and upper sets induced by indiscernibility, or more generally, by granulation relations in data tables. This perspective captures vagueness caused by limited observational resolution and supports set-theoretic reasoning about what can be determined with certainty and what remains only possible. This book is written as a map of models. Rather than developing a single algorithmic pipeline in depth, it provides a systematic survey of the main rough set paradigms and their extension routes. More specifically, representative variants are organized according to (i) the underlying granulation mechanism, such as equivalence-based, tolerance-based, covering-based, neighborhood-based, and probabilistic approximations, and (ii) the uncertainty semantics attached to data and relations, such as crisp, fuzzy, intuitionistic fuzzy, neutrosophic, and plithogenic settings. The book also explains how each choice changes the form of approximations and the interpretation of boundary regions. Throughout the book, small illustrative examples are used to clarify modeling intent and typical use cases in classification and decision support. Finally, an important clarification of scope should be noted. Since the main purpose of this book is to provide a map of models, the Abstract and Introduction should not lead readers to expect that feature reduction and rule induction are primary objectives. Although these topics are central in the rough set literature, they are treated here mainly as motivating applications and as entry points to the broader research landscape. The principal aim of the book is to survey and position rough set models and their extensions in a systematic and coherent manner.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [AtomicRAG: Atom-Entity Graphs for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20844)
*Yanning Hou,Duanyang Yuan,Sihang Zhou,Xiaoshu Chen,Ke Liang,Siwei Wang,Xinwang Liu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AtomicRAG框架，通过将知识表示为原子化的事实单元（知识原子）替代传统基于文本块的粗粒度表示，并利用个性化PageRank与相关性过滤结合的实体连接机制，解决现有GraphRAG中知识表示粒度过粗和关系抽取错误传播的问题，在5个公开基准数据集上验证了检索精度和推理鲁棒性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG方法存在两个关键缺陷：①将文本块作为基本知识表示单元导致多个原子事实被刚性分组，无法灵活适配多样化查询场景；②基于三元组的实体链接对关系抽取错误敏感，容易产生缺失或错误的推理路径，最终损害检索准确性。

Method: ①提出原子-实体图（Atom-Entity Graph）架构，将知识表示为独立的、自包含的事实单元"知识原子"而非粗粒度文本块，实现知识元素的灵活重组与查询视角的对齐；②采用边仅表示实体间是否存在关系的简化图结构；③结合个性化PageRank算法与相关性过滤机制，在保持准确实体连接的同时提升推理可靠性。

Result: 在5个公开基准数据集上的实验表明，AtomicRAG算法在检索精度和推理鲁棒性方面均优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: 原子化的知识表示与可靠的实体连接机制能够有效提升RAG系统的检索与推理能力，为构建更灵活、更鲁棒的检索增强生成系统提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Recent GraphRAG methods integrate graph structures into text indexing and retrieval, using knowledge graph triples to connect text chunks, thereby improving retrieval coverage and precision. However, we observe that treating text chunks as the basic unit of knowledge representation rigidly groups multiple atomic facts together, limiting the flexibility and adaptability needed to support diverse retrieval scenarios. Additionally, triple-based entity linking is sensitive to relation-extraction errors, which can lead to missing or incorrect reasoning paths and ultimately hurt retrieval accuracy. To address these issues, we propose the Atom-Entity Graph, a more precise and reliable architecture for knowledge representation and indexing. In our approach, knowledge is stored as knowledge atoms, namely individual, self-contained units of factual information, rather than coarse-grained text chunks. This allows knowledge elements to be flexibly reassembled without mutual interference, thereby enabling seamless alignment with diverse query perspectives. Edges between entities simply indicate whether a relationship exists. By combining personalized PageRank with relevance-based filtering, we maintain accurate entity connections and improve the reliability of reasoning. Theoretical analysis and experiments on five public benchmarks show that the proposed AtomicRAG algorithm outperforms strong RAG baselines in retrieval accuracy and reasoning robustness. Code: https://github.com/7HHHHH/AtomicRAG.

</details>


### [194] [Caesar: Deep Agentic Web Exploration for Creative Answer Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20855)
*Jason Liang,Elliot Meyerson,Risto Miikkulainen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Caesar是一种面向创意发现的智能体LLM架构，通过知识图谱支持联想推理，结合动态上下文感知探索策略与对抗性草案精炼循环，实现新视角的主动发现，在创造力任务上超越现有最先进智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体框架优先考虑收敛式搜索（convergent search），往往导致生成的结果缺乏创造性，多为衍生性摘要。被动检索无法满足从创意发现角度生成新思想的需求，自主智能体需要深层次联想综合能力。

Method: 包含两大核心组件：(1)由动态上下文感知策略驱动的探索机制；(2)由对抗性草案精炼循环控制的综合机制，主动寻求新视角而非确认既有认知。

Result: Caesar能够生成具有高度新颖性和结构一致性的成果，在需要创造力的任务上显著超越当前最先进的LLM研究智能体。

Conclusion: Caesar显著超越了当前最先进的LLM研究智能体，在需要创造力的任务上表现优异，能够生成具有高度新颖性和结构一致性的成果。

Abstract: To advance from passive retrieval to creative discovery of new ideas, autonomous agents must be capable of deep, associative synthesis. However, current agentic frameworks prioritize convergent search, often resulting in derivative summaries that lack creativity. Caesar is an agentic LLM architecture designed to bridge the gap between information gathering and synthesis of new insights. Unlike existing agents that treat the web as a flat sequence of disconnected documents, Caesar leverages an extensive knowledge graph to foster associative reasoning, thus enabling the discovery of non-obvious connections between disparate concepts. It consists of two components: (1) exploration driven by a dynamic context-aware policy, and (2) synthesis controlled by an adversarial draft refinement loop that actively seeks novel perspectives rather than confirming established priors. Caesar demonstrates the ability to generate artifacts and answers characterized by high novelty and structural coherence, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art LLM research agents in tasks requiring creativity.

</details>


### [195] [CaST-POI: Candidate-Conditioned Spatiotemporal Modeling for Next POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20845)
*Zhenyu Yu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Mohd Yamani Idna Idris,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CaST-POI是一种候选条件时空模型，用于下一个POI推荐。通过让同一用户历史根据不同候选POI被差异化解读，解决了传统方法中候选无关表示的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法从历史轨迹计算单一用户表示，对所有候选POI统一评分，忽略了历史访问的相关性本质上取决于正在评估的候选POI这一事实。

Method: 提出候选条件序列阅读器，用候选POI作为查询动态注意力机制处理用户历史；引入候选相对时空偏差以捕捉细粒度移动模式。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上实验表明，CaST-POI一致优于现有方法，在多个评估指标上取得实质性提升，在大规模候选池下优势尤为突出。

Conclusion: CaST-POI通过候选条件机制解决了传统POI推荐中用户历史表示单一化的问题，在大规模候选池场景下优势显著。代码已开源。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation plays a crucial role in location-based services by predicting users' future mobility patterns. Existing methods typically compute a single user representation from historical trajectories and use it to score all candidate POIs uniformly. However, this candidate-agnostic paradigm overlooks that the relevance of historical visits inherently depends on which candidate is being evaluated. In this paper, we propose CaST-POI, a candidate-conditioned spatiotemporal model for next POI recommendation. Our key insight is that the same user history should be interpreted differently when evaluating different candidate POIs. CaST-POI employs a candidate-conditioned sequence reader that uses candidates as queries to dynamically attend to user history. In addition, we introduce candidate-relative temporal and spatial biases to capture fine-grained mobility patterns based on the relationships between historical visits and each candidate POI. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that CaST-POI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, yielding substantial improvements across multiple evaluation metrics, with particularly strong advantages under large candidate pools. Code is available at https://github.com/YuZhenyuLindy/CaST-POI.git.

</details>


### [196] [ADS-POI: Agentic Spatiotemporal State Decomposition for Next Point-of-Interest Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20846)
*Zhenyu Yu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Mohd Yamani Idna Idris,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ADS-POI，一种用于下一POI推荐的时空状态分解框架。该框架通过多并行潜子状态表示用户行为，每个子状态拥有独立的时空转换动态，并通过上下文条件机制选择性聚合形成决策状态。实验表明该方法显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将用户历史压缩为单一潜在表示，容易纠缠异质信号（常规移动模式、短期意图、时间规律性）。这种纠缠限制了状态演化的灵活性，降低了模型适应多样化决策上下文的能力。

Method: 提出ADS-POI框架，采用多并行潜子状态表示用户，每个子状态由独立的时空转换动态驱动。通过上下文条件机制选择性聚合子状态形成决策状态用于预测。这种设计允许不同行为成分（如常规移动模式、短期意图、时间规律性）在当前时空上下文下以不同速率演化同时保持协调。

Result: 在Foursquare和Gowalla三个真实基准数据集上，ADS-POI在完整排序评估协议下始终优于强基线方法。实验表明将用户行为分解为多个时空感知状态能带来更有效且鲁棒的下一POI推荐。

Conclusion: ADS-POI通过将用户行为解耦为多个时空感知的并行子状态，实现了更灵活、更有效的下一POI推荐。该框架的核心贡献在于提出了上下文条件聚合机制，使得不同行为成分能够在保持协调的同时以不同速率演化。三组真实数据集的实验验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Next point-of-interest (POI) recommendation requires modeling user mobility as a spatiotemporal sequence, where different behavioral factors may evolve at different temporal and spatial scales. Most existing methods compress a user's history into a single latent representation, which tends to entangle heterogeneous signals such as routine mobility patterns, short-term intent, and temporal regularities. This entanglement limits the flexibility of state evolution and reduces the model's ability to adapt to diverse decision contexts. We propose ADS-POI, a spatiotemporal state decomposition framework for next POI recommendation. ADS-POI represents a user with multiple parallel evolving latent sub-states, each governed by its own spatiotemporal transition dynamics. These sub-states are selectively aggregated through a context-conditioned mechanism to form the decision state used for prediction. This design enables different behavioral components to evolve at different rates while remaining coordinated under the current spatiotemporal context. Extensive experiments on three real-world benchmark datasets from Foursquare and Gowalla demonstrate that ADS-POI consistently outperforms strong state-of-the-art baselines under a full-ranking evaluation protocol. The results show that decomposing user behavior into multiple spatiotemporally aware states leads to more effective and robust next POI recommendation. Our code is available at https://github.com/YuZhenyuLindy/ADS-POI.git.

</details>


### [197] [Revisiting Content-Based Music Recommendation: Efficient Feature Aggregation from Large-Scale Music Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20847)
*Yizhi Zhou,Jia-Qi Yang,De-Chuan Zhan,Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TASTE是一个全面的音乐推荐数据集和基准测试框架，集成了音频和文本多模态信息，利用大规模自监督音乐编码器提取音频表示，提出MuQ-token方法高效整合多层音频特征，在候选召回和CTR任务上验证了内容驱动方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐推荐模型主要依赖协同过滤，未能充分利用音频的内在特征，在冷启动场景下表现欠佳。同时，现有音乐推荐数据集缺乏丰富的多模态信息（原始音频信号和描述性文本元数据），评估框架也不够完善，无法支持多样化的算法尤其是多模态方法。

Method: 1) 构建TASTE数据集，集成音频和文本两种模态；2) 利用大规模自监督音乐编码器提取音频表示；3) 提出MuQ-token方法，实现多层音频特征的高效整合。

Result: 提取的音频表示在候选召回和CTR等推荐任务中展现出显著价值；MuQ-token方法在多种设置下始终优于其他特征整合技术。

Conclusion: 研究验证了内容驱动方法的有效性，提供了一个高度有效且可复用的多模态基础框架，可用于未来音乐推荐研究。

Abstract: Music Recommendation Systems (MRSs) are a cornerstone of modern streaming platforms. Existing recommendation models, spanning both recall and ranking stages, predominantly rely on collaborative filtering, which fails to exploit the intrinsic characteristics of audio and consequently leads to suboptimal performance, particularly in cold-start scenarios. However, existing music recommendation datasets often lack rich multimodal information, such as raw audio signals and descriptive textual metadata. Moreover, current recommender system evaluation frameworks remain inadequate, as they neither fully leverage multimodal information nor support a diverse range of algorithms, especially multimodal methods. To address these limitations, we propose TASTE, a comprehensive dataset and benchmarking framework designed to highlight the role of multimodal information in music recommendation. Our dataset integrates both audio and textual modalities. By leveraging recent large-scale self-supervised music encoders, we demonstrate the substantial value of the extracted audio representations across recommendation tasks, including candidate recall and CTR. In addition, we introduce the \textbf{MuQ-token} method, which enables more efficient integration of multi-layer audio features. This method consistently outperforms other feature integration techniques across various settings. Overall, our results not only validate the effectiveness of content-driven approaches but also provide a highly effective and reusable multimodal foundation for future research. Code is available at https://github.com/zreach/TASTE

</details>


### [198] [MATRAG: Multi-Agent Transparent Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Explainable Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20848)
*Sushant Mehta*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MATRAG proposes a multi-agent transparent RAG framework for explainable LLM-based recommendations, combining four specialized agents with knowledge graph augmentation to achieve state-of-the-art accuracy and high-quality explanations, demonstrating practical potential for production deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM-based recommendation systems face critical challenges in transparency, knowledge grounding, and the ability to provide coherent explanations that foster user trust.

Method: MATRAG employs four specialized agents: (1) User Modeling Agent for dynamic preference profiles, (2) Item Analysis Agent for semantic feature extraction from knowledge graphs, (3) Reasoning Agent for synthesizing collaborative and content-based signals, and (4) Explanation Agent for generating knowledge-grounded natural language justifications. The framework incorporates a transparency scoring mechanism to quantify explanation faithfulness and relevance.

Result: On three benchmark datasets (Amazon Reviews, MovieLens-1M, Yelp), MATRAG improves recommendation accuracy by 12.7% (Hit Rate) and 15.3% (NDCG) over leading baselines, with 87.4% of generated explanations rated as helpful and trustworthy by domain experts.

Conclusion: MATRAG establishes new benchmarks for transparent, agentic recommendation systems, achieving state-of-the-art performance with significant improvements in accuracy metrics (12.7% Hit Rate, 15.3% NDCG) and explanation quality (87.4% helpful ratings), while providing actionable insights for production deployment of LLM-based recommenders.

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based recommendation systems have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in understanding user preferences and generating personalized suggestions. However, existing approaches face critical challenges in transparency, knowledge grounding, and the ability to provide coherent explanations that foster user trust. We introduce MATRAG (Multi-Agent Transparent Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a novel framework that combined multi-agent collaboration with knowledge graph-augmented retrieval to deliver explainable recommendations. MATRAG employs four specialized agents: a User Modeling Agent that constructs dynamic preference profiles, an Item Analysis Agent that extracts semantic features from knowledge graphs, a Reasoning Agent that synthesizes collaborative and content-based signals, and an Explanation Agent that generates natural language justifications grounded in retrieved knowledge. Our framework incorporates a transparency scoring mechanism that quantifies explanation faithfulness and relevance. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets (Amazon Reviews, MovieLens-1M, and Yelp) demonstrate that MATRAG achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving recommendation accuracy by 12.7\% (Hit Rate) and 15.3\% (NDCG) over leading baselines, while human evaluation confirms that 87.4\% of generated explanations are rated as helpful and trustworthy by domain experts. Our work establishes new benchmarks for transparent, agentic recommendation systems and provides actionable insights for deploying LLM-based recommenders in production environments.

</details>


### [199] [SPIRE: Structure-Preserving Interpretable Retrieval of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20849)
*Mike Rainey,Umut Acar,Muhammed Sezer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对半结构化文档（如HTML）的检索增强生成存在文档结构与平面嵌入模型之间的不匹配问题。本文提出结构感知检索管道，通过"子文档"表示和双重上下文机制（全局/局部上下文）解决固定分块导致结构信息丢失的问题，在HTML问答基准上实现了更高质量和多样性的引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索管道将文档线性化为固定大小块，破坏了HTML等半结构化文档的章节结构、列表和表格信息，导致难以返回可引用的小粒度证据，同时保留使其可解释的周围上下文。

Method: 提出基于树结构文档的结构感知检索管道。核心概念是将候选表示为保留结构标识的"子文档"。定义了文档基元：路径和路径集、剪枝子文档提取、两层上下文机制（全局上下文添加非本地脚手架；局部上下文在结构邻域内扩展）、基于嵌入的候选生成器（索引句子级子文档）和文档感知聚合步骤，以及利用本地上下文视图重新排序的上下文过滤阶段。

Result: 在HTML问答基准测试中，保留结构并对选择进行上下文处理的方法在固定预算下产生更高质量、更多样化的引用，同时保持了可扩展性，优于强基线的基于段落方法。

Conclusion: 结构感知方法能够有效平衡检索粒度与上下文完整性，为半结构化文档的检索增强生成提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over semi-structured sources such as HTML is constrained by a mismatch between document structure and the flat, sequence-based interfaces of today's embedding and generative models. Retrieval pipelines often linearize documents into fixed-size chunks before indexing, which obscures section structure, lists, and tables, and makes it difficult to return small, citation-ready evidence without losing the surrounding context that makes it interpretable.
  We present a structure-aware retrieval pipeline that operates over tree-structured documents. The core idea is to represent candidates as subdocuments: precise, addressable selections that preserve structural identity while deferring the choice of surrounding context. We define a small set of document primitives--paths and path sets, subdocument extraction by pruning, and two contextualization mechanisms. Global contextualization adds the non-local scaffolding needed to make a selection intelligible (e.g., titles, headers, list and table structure). Local contextualization expands a seed selection within its structural neighborhood to obtain a compact, context-rich view under a target budget. Building on these primitives, we describe an embedding-based candidate generator that indexes sentence-seeded subdocuments and a query-time, document-aware aggregation step that amortizes shared structural context. We then introduce a contextual filtering stage that re-scores retrieved candidates using locally contextualized views.
  Across experiments on HTML question-answering benchmarks, we find that preserving structure while contextualizing selections yields higher-quality, more diverse citations under fixed budgets than strong passage-based baselines, while maintaining scalability.

</details>


### [200] [Association Is Not Similarity: Learning Corpus-Specific Associations for Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20850)
*Jason Dury*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AAR方法，通过对比学习训练MLP在嵌入空间学习段落关联，用于稠密检索系统的转导式重排序。该方法在多跳问答数据集上显著提升检索性能，尤其对困难问题效果明显，但主要捕捉语料库特定的共现模式而非可迁移规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索系统虽能通过嵌入相似度对段落进行排序，但在处理多跳问题时表现不佳。多跳问题需要通过共享推理链关联的段落，而现有稠密检索方法难以捕捉这种关联关系。

Method: 提出Association-Augmented Retrieval (AAR)方法，一种轻量级的转导式重排序方法。核心是一个4.2M参数的MLP模型，通过对比学习利用共现注释在嵌入空间中学习段落间的关联关系。推理时使用双向关联评分对初始稠密检索候选集进行重排序。

Result: 在HotpotQA上，段落Recall@5从0.831提升至0.916（+8.6点），在困难问题上增益达+28.5点；在MuSiQue转导式设置下提升+10.1点。归纳式设置（训练集关联→验证集关联）无显著提升，表明方法捕捉的是语料库特定的共现而非可迁移模式。下游QA任务中检索增益转化为+6.4点的Exact Match提升。

Conclusion: 该方法有效捕捉语料库特定的共现关系而非可迁移的模式。消融实验表明：语义相似但无关联的段落对会降低检索性能，而打乱关联对会导致严重性能下降。在效率方面，该方法每查询仅增加3.7ms延迟，单GPU训练时间不足2分钟，无需基于LLM的索引构建。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems rank passages by embedding similarity to a query, but multi-hop questions require passages that are associatively related through shared reasoning chains. We introduce Association-Augmented Retrieval (AAR), a lightweight transductive reranking method that trains a small MLP (4.2M parameters) to learn associative relationships between passages in embedding space using contrastive learning on co-occurrence annotations. At inference time, AAR reranks an initial dense retrieval candidate set using bi-directional association scoring. On HotpotQA, AAR improves passage Recall@5 from 0.831 to 0.916 (+8.6 points) without evaluation-set tuning, with gains concentrated on hard questions where the dense baseline fails (+28.5 points). On MuSiQue, AAR achieves +10.1 points in the transductive setting. An inductive model trained on training-split associations and evaluated on unseen validation associations shows no significant improvement, suggesting that the method captures corpus-specific co-occurrences rather than transferable patterns. Ablation studies support this interpretation: training on semantically similar but non-associated passage pairs degrades retrieval below the baseline, while shuffling association pairs causes severe degradation. A downstream QA evaluation shows retrieval gains translate to +6.4 exact match improvement. The method adds 3.7ms per query, trains in under two minutes on a single GPU, and requires no LLM-based indexing.

</details>


### [201] [Robust Test-time Video-Text Retrieval: Benchmarking and Adapting for Query Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20851)
*Bingqing Zhang,Zhuo Cao,Heming Du,Yang Li,Xue Li,Jiajun Liu,Sen Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对现代视频-文本检索（VTR）模型在真实世界查询偏移场景下性能急剧下降的问题，提出系统性的基准测试和基于测试时自适应的解决方案HAT-VTR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTR模型在分布内基准测试表现优异，但在真实世界查询偏移场景下（查询数据分布偏离训练域）鲁棒性极差。现有图像领域的鲁棒性方法无法有效处理视频中复杂的时空动态变化，因此需要专门针对视频查询偏移的系统性评估和解决方案。

Method: 1) 构建综合基准测试：12种视频扰动类型 × 5个严重程度等级；2) 发现核心问题：查询偏移会显著放大"hubness现象"（少数gallery项目成为主导"hub"，吸引不成比例的查询）；3) 提出HAT-VTR框架（Hubness Alleviation for Test-time Video-Text Retrieval），包含两个关键组件：Hubness Suppression Memory（抑制hubness的记忆机制）和Multi-granular Losses（多粒度损失函数用于强制时序特征一致性）。

Result: HAT-VTR在多种查询偏移场景下均显著优于现有方法，有效提升了模型的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统性地揭示了VTR中查询偏移放大的hubness问题，并提出了有效的测试时自适应解决方案，为视频-文本检索模型在真实世界应用中的部署提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Modern video-text retrieval (VTR) models excel on in-distribution benchmarks but are highly vulnerable to real-world query shifts, where the distribution of query data deviates from the training domain, leading to a sharp performance drop. Existing image-focused robustness solutions are inadequate to handle this vulnerability in video, as they fail to address the complex spatio-temporal dynamics inherent in these shifts. To systematically evaluate this vulnerability, we first introduce a comprehensive benchmark featuring 12 distinct types of video perturbations across five severity degrees. Analysis on this benchmark reveals that query shifts amplify the hubness phenomenon, where a few gallery items become dominant "hubs" that attract a disproportionate number of queries. To mitigate this, we then propose HAT-VTR (Hubness Alleviation for Test-time Video-Text Retrieval), as our baseline test-time adaptation framework designed to directly counteract hubness in VTR. It leverages two key components: a Hubness Suppression Memory to refine similarity scores, and multi-granular losses to enforce temporal feature consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HAT-VTR substantially improves robustness, consistently outperforming prior methods across diverse query shift scenarios, and enhancing model reliability for real-world applications.

</details>


### [202] [DenoiseRank: Learning to Rank by Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20852)
*Ying Wang,Preslav Nakov,Shangsong Liang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DenoiseRank，首个从生成视角解决排序问题的深度扩散模型，通过对相关标签加噪并在反向过程中去噪来预测文档分布，在基准数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Learning to Rank (LTR)模型均从判别视角出发，尚未有生成式方法解决排序任务。作者希望探索一种全新的生成式排序范式。

Method: 提出DenoiseRank模型，采用扩散模型框架：1) 在前向扩散过程中对相关标签添加噪声；2) 在反向去噪过程中对查询-文档进行去噪，从而准确预测其分布。

Result: DenoiseRank作为首个生成式LTR方法和首个LTR扩散模型，在多个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 生成式排序方法是可行的，DenoiseRank为生成式LTR任务提供了基准，为排序学习开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Learning to rank (LTR) is one of the core tasks in Machine Learning. Traditional LTR models have made great progress, but nearly all of them are implemented from discriminative perspective. In this paper, we aim at addressing LTR from a novel perspective, i.e., by a deep generative model. Specifically, we propose a novel denoise rank model, DenoiseRank, which noises the relevant labels in the diffusion process and denoises them on the query documents in the reverse process to accurately predict their distribution. Our model is the first to address traditional LTR from generative perspective and is a diffusion method for LTR. Our extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrated the effectiveness of DenoiseRank, and we believe it provides a benchmark for generative LTR task.

</details>


### [203] [A Systematic Study of Biomedical Retrieval Pipeline Trade-offs in Performance and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20853)
*Hayk Stepanyan,Matthew McDermott*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文通过大规模实证研究，系统评估了生物医学检索管道设计选择对性能和效率的影响，为研究者提供了包括语料库聚合策略、HNSW索引下MedRAG/pubmed的Pareto最优性、chunking策略及FAISS索引权衡在内的具体实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学和临床NLP应用中检索系统的使用日益增加，但研究者构建此类系统时缺乏实践指导。

Method: 通过在多种公共生物医学文本数据集上进行实验，系统性地评估不同检索管道设置（语料库选择、chunk粒度、向量索引配置），使用LLM-as-a-judge框架结合人工验证进行win-rate评估。

Result: 发现语料库聚合对绝对检索质量的优势；MedRAG/pubmed在基于图（HNSW）索引下成为Pareto最优单语料库；确定了合适的chunking策略和FAISS索引的最佳速度效率权衡方案。

Conclusion: 研究为构建生物医学检索系统的研究者提供了可操作的建议，包括语料库聚合策略的选择、MedRAG/pubmed在HNSW索引下的Pareto最优性、chunking策略以及FAISS索引的效率权衡建议。

Abstract: Retrieval systems are increasingly used in biomedical and clinical natural language processing applications, yet practical guidance for researchers building such systems is limited. In this work, we provide such guidance through an empirical study of how retrieval pipeline design choices affect performance and efficiency at scale.
  In particular, we examine retrieval over a variety of existing, public biomedical text datasets, leveraging a variety of disparate types of queries, including exam-style questions, conversational medical queries, community-asked questions, and non-question formulations across various retrieval pipeline settings spanning corpus selection, chunk granularity, and vector index configuration. Retrieval results are judged using a robust, win-rate comparison assessment via an LLM-as-a-judge setting with human validation.
  Across these experiments, we identify several points of concrete guidance for reviewers, including the superiority of corpus aggregation for absolute retrieval quality, and the emergence of MedRAG/pubmed as the Pareto-optimal singleton corpus under graph-based (HNSW) indexing, appropriate chunking strategies, and FAISS indexing choices that offer the best trade-offs in speed and efficiency.

</details>


### [204] [ERA: Evidence-based Reliability Alignment for Honest Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20854)
*Sunguk Shin,Meeyoung Cha,Byung-Jun Lee,Sungwon Park*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ERA框架，通过将标量置信度估计转向显式证据分布来增强RAG系统的弃权行为。核心创新在于利用Dirichlet分布建模知识证据和DST理论量化冲突，从而解耦认知不确定性和随机不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成(RAG)系统面临内部化参数知识与检索信息之间的知识冲突问题，而现有可靠性方法主要依赖标量置信度，无法显式区分认知不确定性(epistemic uncertainty)和数据固有模糊性(aleatoric uncertainty)。

Method: 提出ERA框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)上下文证据量化，使用Dirichlet分布将内部参数知识和外部检索知识建模为独立信念质量；(2)基于Dempster-Shafer Theory(DST)量化信息源间的几何不一致性。该框架通过证据分布而非标量概率进行置信度估计，实现认知不确定性和随机不确定性的解耦，并根据检测到的冲突动态调制优化目标。

Result: 在标准基准测试和精选的泛化数据集上，该方法显著优于基线方法，在优化答案覆盖率和弃权权衡方面展现出更优的校准性能。

Conclusion: 实验表明该方法显著优于基线方法，在答案覆盖率和弃权之间实现了更优的权衡，展现出更优越的校准性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds language models in factual evidence but introduces critical challenges regarding knowledge conflicts between internalized parameters and retrieved information. However, existing reliability methods, typically relying on scalar confidence, fail to explicitly distinguish between epistemic uncertainty and inherent data ambiguity in such hybrid scenarios. In this paper, we propose a new framework called ERA (Evidence-based Reliability Alignment) to enhance abstention behavior in RAG systems by shifting confidence estimation from scalar probabilities to explicit evidence distributions. Our method consists of two main components: (1) Contextual Evidence Quantification, which models internal and external knowledge as independent belief masses via the Dirichlet distribution, and (2) Quantifying Knowledge Conflict, which leverages Dempster-Shafer Theory (DST) to rigorously measure the geometric discordance between information sources. These components are used to disentangle epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric uncertainty and modulate the optimization objective based on detected conflicts. Experiments on standard benchmarks and a curated generalization dataset demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms baselines, optimizing the trade-off between answer coverage and abstention with superior calibration.

</details>


### [205] [DiagramBank: A Large-scale Dataset of Diagram Design Exemplars with Paper Metadata for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20857)
*Tingwen Zhang,Ling Yue,Zhen Xu,Shaowu Pan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DiagramBank是一个大规模科学图表数据集（89,422张示意图），用于多模态检索和示例驱动的科学图表生成，旨在解决AI科学家系统在生成论文级Teaser图方面的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI科学家系统可自动撰写论文和代码，但生成出版物级别的科学图表（如Teaser图）仍是端到端论文生成流程的主要瓶颈。Teaser图需要概念性综合和规划，将复杂逻辑工作流转化为引导直觉、激发好奇心的图像，而现有系统通常省略或采用劣质替代方案。

Method: 通过自动化筛选流程开发：从现有顶级科学出版物中提取图表和文内引用，使用CLIP-based过滤器区分示意图与标准图表或自然图像；每个实例配有丰富的上下文信息（摘要、标题、图表-引用对），支持不同查询粒度的信息检索。

Result: 构建了包含89,422张示意图的大规模数据集，支持多模态检索和示例条件下的Teaser图合成，提供可索引格式和检索增强生成代码库。

Conclusion: DiagramBank填补了AI科学家系统在科学可视化组件方面的空白，为端到端自动化科学论文生成提供了重要支持。

Abstract: Recent advances in autonomous ``AI scientist'' systems have demonstrated the ability to automatically write scientific manuscripts and codes with execution. However, producing a publication-grade scientific diagram (e.g., teaser figure) is still a major bottleneck in the ``end-to-end'' paper generation process. For example, a teaser figure acts as a strategic visual interface and serves a different purpose than derivative data plots. It demands conceptual synthesis and planning to translate complex logic workflow into a compelling graphic that guides intuition and sparks curiosity. Existing AI scientist systems usually omit this component or fall back to an inferior alternative. To bridge this gap, we present DiagramBank, a large-scale dataset consisting of 89,422 schematic diagrams curated from existing top-tier scientific publications, designed for multimodal retrieval and exemplar-driven scientific figure generation. DiagramBank is developed through our automated curation pipeline that extracts figures and corresponding in-text references, and uses a CLIP-based filter to differentiate schematic diagrams from standard plots or natural images. Each instance is paired with rich context from abstract, caption, to figure-reference pairs, enabling information retrieval under different query granularities. We release DiagramBank in a ready-to-index format and provide a retrieval-augmented generation codebase to demonstrate exemplar-conditioned synthesis of teaser figures. DiagramBank is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/zhangt20/DiagramBank with code at https://github.com/csml-rpi/DiagramBank.

</details>


### [206] [Mixture of Sequence: Theme-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20858)
*Xiao Lin,Zhicheng Tang,Weilin Cong,Mengyue Hang,Kai Wang,Yajuan Wang,Zhichen Zeng,Ting-Wei Li,Hyunsik Yoo,Zhining Liu,Xuying Ning,Ruizhong Qiu,Wen-yen Chen,Shuo Chang,Rong Jin,Huayu Li,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MoS（Mixture of Sequence）框架解决sequential recommendation中长序列建模的挑战。通过识别"session hopping"用户行为模式，设计了theme-aware routing mechanism将序列按主题分割成多个连贯子序列以过滤噪音，以及multi-scale fusion mechanism利用三类experts捕获不同粒度的信息。最终在保持SOTA推荐性能的同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sequential recommendation面临长序列建模的核心挑战：用户存在显著的interest shifts，导致序列中包含大量不相关甚至误导性的信息。通过实证分析发现用户行为存在"session hopping"模式——在短时间跨度内兴趣保持稳定（session内），但跨session时会急剧转变，且可能在多个session后再次出现。

Method: MoS采用了两阶段方案：1) Theme-aware routing mechanism：自适应学习用户序列的潜在主题，将原始序列组织成多个主题对齐的子序列，每个子序列只包含与特定主题相关的sessions，从而过滤掉interest shifts引入的噪音信息。2) Multi-scale fusion mechanism：引入三种类型的experts分别捕获全局序列特征、短期用户行为和主题特定语义模式，实现多尺度信息融合。

Result: MoS在多个基准数据集上实现了SOTA性能，同时相比其他MoE方法引入了更少的FLOPs，证明了其在utility和efficiency之间的出色平衡。

Conclusion: MoS提出了一种优雅的解决方案，通过theme-aware routing和multi-scale fusion两大机制，实现了在长序列建模中的精确推荐。实验证明该方法在保持SOTA性能的同时，显著降低了计算成本，为sequential recommendation领域提供了一种utility和efficiency良好平衡的MoE范式。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation has rapidly advanced in click-through rate prediction due to its ability to model dynamic user interests. A key challenge, however, lies in modeling long sequences: users often exhibit significant interest shifts, introducing substantial irrelevant or misleading information. Our empirical analysis corroborates this challenge and uncovers a recurring behavioral pattern in long sequences (\textit{session hopping}): user interests remain stable within short temporal spans (\textit{sessions}) but shift drastically across sessions and may reappear after multiple sessions. To address this challenge, we propose the Mixture of Sequence (MoS) framework, a model-agnostic MoE approach that achieves accurate predictions by extracting theme-specific and multi-scale subsequences from noisy raw user sequences. First, MoS employs a theme-aware routing mechanism to adaptively learn the latent themes of user sequences and organizes these sequences into multiple coherent subsequences. Each subsequence contains only sessions aligned with a specific theme, thereby effectively filtering out irrelevant or even misleading information introduced by user interest shifts in session hopping. In addition, to alleviate potential information loss, we introduce a multi-scale fusion mechanism, which leverages three types of experts to capture global sequence characteristics, short-term user behaviors, and theme-specific semantic patterns. Together, these two mechanisms endow MoS with the ability to deliver accurate recommendations from multi-faceted and multi-scale perspectives. Experimental results demonstrate that MoS consistently achieves the SOTA performance while introducing fewer FLOPs compared with other MoE counterparts, providing strong evidence of its excellent balance between utility and efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/xiaolin-cs/MoS.

</details>


### [207] [KGiRAG: An Iterative GraphRAG Approach for Responding Sensemaking Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20859)
*Isabela Iacob,Melisa Marian,Gheorghe Cosmin Silaghi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图检索增强的迭代反馈架构，通过响应质量评估循环优化输出，解决LLM幻觉和上下文不足问题，在HotPotQA数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在幻觉问题，且在处理足够大的上下文以支撑复杂查询方面存在技术局限。

Method: 提出一种迭代式、反馈驱动的GraphRAG架构，利用响应质量评估来迭代优化输出，直至产生可靠且有据可查的响应。使用HotPotQA数据集进行评估。

Result: 该迭代式RAG策略产生的响应相比单次基线方法具有更高的语义质量和更好的相关性。

Conclusion: 该迭代式反馈驱动的GraphRAG架构为解决LLM幻觉和上下文限制问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent literature highlights the potential of graph-based approaches within large language model (LLM) retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines for answering queries of varying complexity, particularly those that fall outside the LLM's prior knowledge. However, LLMs are prone to hallucination and often face technical limitations in handling contexts large enough to ground complex queries effectively. To address these challenges, we propose a novel iterative, feedback-driven GraphRAG architecture that leverages response quality assessment to iteratively refine outputs until a sound, well-grounded response is produced. Evaluating our approach with queries from the HotPotQA dataset, we demonstrate that this iterative RAG strategy yields responses with higher semantic quality and improved relevance compared to a single-shot baseline.

</details>


### [208] [RealRoute: Dynamic Query Routing System via Retrieve-then-Verify Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20860)
*Jiahe Liu,Qinkai Yu,Jingcheng Niu,Xi Zhu,Zirui He,Zhen Xiang,Fan Yang,Jinman Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RealRoute提出了一种从预测式路由到"检索-验证"范式的转变，通过并行、源无关的检索和动态验证器来解决异构数据源RAG应用中的路由错误问题，显著提升了多跳推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统使用"LLM-as-a-Router"策略进行预测式路由，但该方法过度依赖数据源的语义含义，当源边界模糊时容易导致路由错误，难以有效处理异构数据源（如私有数据库、全文语料库和API）场景。

Method: RealRoute采用"Retrieve-then-Verify"机制：首先通过并行、源无关的方式进行检索以确保证据完整性；然后使用动态验证器对结果进行交叉检查；最后综合生成事实性答案。

Result: RealRoute在多跳RAG推理任务中显著优于预测式基线方法。系统提供可视化界面展示实时重路由过程和跨多知识库的验证链。

Conclusion: RealRoute通过从预测式路由范式向检索-验证范式的转变，有效解决了异构数据源RAG应用中的路由挑战，提升了答案的可靠性和准确性。

Abstract: Despite the success of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) in grounding LLMs with external knowledge, its application over heterogeneous sources (e.g., private databases, global corpora, and APIs) remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches typically employ an LLM-as-a-Router to dispatch decomposed sub-queries to specific sources in a predictive manner. However, this "LLM-as-a-Router" strategy relies heavily on the semantic meaning of different data sources, often leading to routing errors when source boundaries are ambiguous. In this work, we introduce RealRoute System, a framework that shifts the paradigm from predictive routing to a robust Retrieve-then-Verify mechanism. RealRoute ensures \textit{evidence completeness through parallel, source-agnostic retrieval, followed by a dynamic verifier that cross-checks the results and synthesizes a factually grounded answer}. Our demonstration allows users to visualize the real-time "re-routing" process and inspect the verification chain across multiple knowledge silos. Experiments show that RealRoute significantly outperforms predictive baselines in the multi-hop Rag reasoning task. The RealRoute system is released as an open-source toolkit with a user-friendly web interface. The code is available at the URL: https://github.com/Joseph1951210/RealRoute.

</details>


### [209] [Deep Interest Mining with Cross-Modal Alignment for SemanticID Generation in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20861)
*Yagchen Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的生成式推荐框架，针对现有语义ID（SID）生成方法存在的信息退化、语义退化和模态失真三大问题，通过视觉-语言模型对齐、深度兴趣挖掘和质量感知强化学习机制三个创新点，显著提升了推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法依赖语义ID将海量数据压缩为可学习的词表序列，但存在三个关键缺陷：(1) 两阶段压缩导致语义丢失且无后验机制区分质量；(2) 级联量化丢弃关键语义信息且两阶段未联合优化；(3) 量化器无法正确对齐文本与图像模态。这些问题严重制约了生成式推荐系统的性能上限。

Method: 提出三个核心创新：(1) DCIM（深度上下文兴趣挖掘）：利用VLMs将非文本模态对齐到统一文本语义空间，通过重建监督保留关键上下文信息；(2) CMSA（跨模态语义对齐）：解决模态失真问题；(3) QARM（质量感知强化机制）：采用质量感知奖励的强化学习框架，鼓励生成语义丰富的SID而抑制低质量SID。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多个基准数据集上持续优于最先进的SID生成方法，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过整合视觉-语言模型对齐、深度兴趣挖掘和质量感知强化学习，该框架有效解决了生成式推荐中语义ID的信息退化、语义退化和模态失真问题，为下一代推荐系统提供了更优的技术方案。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has demonstrated remarkable performance in next-token prediction paradigms, which relies on Semantic IDs (SIDs) to compress trillion-scale data into learnable vocabulary sequences. However, existing methods suffer from three critical limitations: (1) Information Degradation: the two-stage compression pipeline causes semantic loss and information degradation, with no posterior mechanism to distinguish high-quality from low-quality SIDs; (2) Semantic Degradation: cascaded quantization discards key semantic information from original multimodal features, as the embedding generation and quantization stages are not jointly optimized toward a unified objective; (3) Modality Distortion: quantizers fail to properly align text and image modalities, causing feature misalignment even when upstream networks have aligned them. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework integrating three key innovations: Deep Contextual Interest Mining (DCIM), Cross-Modal Semantic Alignment (CMSA), and Quality-Aware Reinforcement Mechanism (QARM). First, we leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to align non-textual modalities into a unified text-based semantic space, mitigating modality distortion. Second, we introduce a deep interest mining mechanism that captures high-level semantic information implicitly present in advertising contexts, encouraging SIDs to preserve critical contextual information through reconstruction-based supervision. Third, we employ a reinforcement learning framework with quality-aware rewards to encourage semantically rich SIDs while suppressing low-quality ones in the posterior stage. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art SID generation methods, achieving superior performance on multiple benchmarks. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each proposed component

</details>


### [210] [Following the Eye-Tracking Evidence: Established Web-Search Assumptions Fail in Carousel Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21019)
*Jingwei Kang,Maarten de Rijke,Harrie Oosterhuis*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究通过眼动追踪数据分析轮播界面用户行为，发现传统Web搜索界面的用户行为假设（如F-pattern和检查假设）并不适用于轮播界面，并提出L-pattern这一新模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮播界面作为流媒体服务的标准界面已使用超过十年，但学界对其用户行为的研究极度匮乏。之前的研究直接将Web搜索界面的行为模式（如F-pattern、检查假设）应用于轮播界面设计点击模型和评估指标，但缺乏实证验证。

Method: 使用眼动追踪研究收集的交互和检查数据集，分析轮播界面上的用户行为模式。

Result: 发现三个关键结论：(i) F-pattern仅适用于垂直浏览而非水平滑动，且在点击条件下用户检查行为呈现轮播界面特有的L-pattern；(ii) 检查条件下的点击率表明经典检查假设在轮播界面不成立；(iii) 与先前假设相反，用户通常忽略轮播标题而直接关注内容项。

Conclusion: Web搜索界面的用户行为假设（尤其是检查模式）不能直接迁移到轮播推荐场景。现有轮播界面的指标和点击模型需要重新评估，亟需建立更可靠的用户行为理论基础。

Abstract: Carousel interfaces have been the de-facto standard for streaming media services for over a decade. Yet, there has been very little research into user behavior with such interfaces, which thus remains poorly understood. Due to this lack of empirical research, previous work has assumed that behaviors established in single-list web-search interfaces, such as the F-pattern and the examination hypothesis, also apply to carousel interfaces, for instance when designing click models or evaluation metrics. We analyze a recently-released interaction and examination dataset resulting from an eye-tracking study performed on carousel interfaces to verify whether these assumptions actually hold.
  We find that (i)~the F-pattern holds only for vertical examination and not for horizontal swiping; additionally, we discover that, when conditioned on a click, user examination follows an L-pattern unique to carousel interfaces; (ii)~click-through-rates conditioned on examination indicate that the well-known examination hypothesis does not hold in carousel interfaces; and (iii)~contrary to the assumptions of previous work, users generally ignore carousel headings and focus directly on the content items. Our findings show that many user behavior assumptions, especially concerning examination patterns, do not transfer from web search interfaces to carousel recommendation settings. Our work shows that the field lacks a reliable foundation on which to build models of user behavior with these interfaces. Consequently, a re-evaluation of existing metrics and click models for carousel interfaces may be warranted.

</details>


### [211] [Automated Extraction of Pharmacokinetic Parameters from Structured XML Scientific Articles: Enhancing Data Accessibility at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21063)
*Remya Ampadi Ramachandran,Lisa A. Tell,Sidharth Rai,Nuwan Millagaha Gedara,Hossein Sholehrasa,Jim E. Riviere,Majid Jaberi-Douraki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出药理学领域PK数据存储分散、提取困难的问题，介绍了FARAD组织40年来手动提取数据的工作面临的人力资源瓶颈，强调开发能够准确识别表格结构、处理行列表头信息的AI自动化算法的重要性和紧迫性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药理学领域缺乏集中、完整、实时的PK数据存储库，导致研究人员需要从分散的XML、HTML、PDF等格式的文献和监管文档中手动提取表格数据，工作量大且易出错。FARAD虽有40余年手动提取经验，但面对每日海量发布的文献，自动化需求日益迫切。

Method: 文中未明确描述具体方法，主要聚焦于问题阐述与需求分析。推断后续将采用基于AI/机器学习的表格检测与提取算法，这些算法需能够准确处理具有复杂行列结构的表格单元格组织方式。

Result: 该文为综述/需求分析类摘要，未报告具体实验结果。主要贡献在于系统阐述PK数据表格提取的现状与挑战，明确指出自动化表格检测与信息提取的必要性。

Conclusion: 开发能够准确理解表格结构、处理行列表头信息并符合人类阅读习惯的AI算法是当前药理学领域数据提取的迫切需求。自动化表格检测与信息提取技术有望显著提高PK数据处理效率，减轻人工负担。

Abstract: In the field of pharmacology, there is a notable absence of centralized, comprehensive, and up-to-date repositories of PK data. This poses a significant challenge for R&D as it can be a time-consuming and challenging task to collect all the required quantitative PK parameters from diverse scientific publications. This quantitative PK information is predominantly organized in tabular format, mostly available as XML, HTML, or PDF files within various online repositories and scientific publications, including supplementary materials. This makes tables one of the crucial components and information elements of scientific or regulatory documents as they are commonly utilized to present quantitative information. Extracting data from tables is typically a labor-intensive process, and alternative automated machine learning models may struggle to accurately detect and extract the relevant data due to the complex nature and diverse layouts of tabular data. The difficulty of information extraction and reading order detection is largely dependent on the structural complexity of the tables. Efforts to understand tables should prioritize capturing the content of table cells in a manner that aligns with how a human reader naturally comprehends the information. FARAD has been manually extracting tabular data and other information from literature and regulatory agencies for over 40 years. However, there is now an urgent need to automate this process due to the large volume of publications released daily. The accuracy of this task has become increasingly challenging, as manual extraction is tedious and prone to errors, especially given the staffing shortages we are currently facing. This necessitates the development of AI algorithms for table detection and extraction that are able to precisely handle cells organized according to the table structure, as indicated by column and/or row header information.

</details>


### [212] [Multilingual and Domain-Agnostic Tip-of-the-Tongue Query Generation for Simulated Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21096)
*Xuhong He,To Eun Kim,Maik Fröbe,Jaime Arguello,Bhaskar Mitra,Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究构建了中文、日文、韩文和英文四种语言的多语言ToT测试集（每种语言5,000条查询），通过LLM模拟查询并验证其与真实用户查询的一致性，发现非英语源的重要性，同时英语Wikipedia可作为辅助信息来源。这是首个大规模多语言ToT基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Tip-of-the-Tongue (ToT)检索基准主要针对英语，限制了其在多语言信息访问场景中的适用性。

Method: 采用基于LLM的查询模拟框架构建多语言ToT测试集；系统研究提示语言和源文档语言对模拟查询保真度的影响；通过系统排序相关性验证合成查询与真实用户查询的一致性。

Result: 有效的ToT模拟需要考虑语言aware的设计选择：非英语源通常较为重要，而当非英语源信息不足时，英语Wikipedia可作为有益补充。

Conclusion: 本研究发布了首个大规模多语言ToT测试集（每种语言5,000条查询），涵盖多个领域，并提供了构建逼真ToT数据集的实践指导。研究结果表明，非英语源通常很重要，而当非英语源信息不足以生成查询时，英语Wikipedia可作为补充来源。

Abstract: Tip-of-the-Tongue (ToT) retrieval benchmarks have largely focused on English, limiting their applicability to multilingual information access. In this work, we construct multilingual ToT test collections for Chinese, Japanese, Korean, and English, using an LLM-based query simulation framework. We systematically study how prompt language and source document language affect the fidelity of simulated ToT queries, validating synthetic queries through system rank correlation against real user queries. Our results show that effective ToT simulation requires language-aware design choices: non-English language sources are generally important, while English Wikipedia can be beneficial when non-English sources provide insufficient information for query generation. Based on these findings, we release four ToT test collections with 5,000 queries per language across multiple domains. This work provides the first large-scale multilingual ToT benchmark and offers practical guidance for constructing realistic ToT datasets beyond English.

</details>


### [213] [PAPERMIND: Benchmarking Agentic Reasoning and Critique over Scientific Papers in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21304)
*Yanjun Zhao,Tianxin Wei,Jiaru Zou,Xuying Ning,Yuanchen Bei,Lingjie Chen,Simmi Rana,Wendy H. Yang,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PAPERMIND是一个用于评估科研论文综合理解和智能体推理能力的基准测试，涵盖农业、生物学、化学、计算机科学、医学、物理和经济学七个领域，包含四个互补的任务族：多模态定位、实验解释、跨源证据推理和批判性评估。实验表明现有多模态LLM在综合科学推理方面仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常孤立评估科学理解能力，难以全面评估科研论文理解这一综合性认知能力。科学理解需要整合文本和视觉信息、解释实验证据、跨源综合信息并批判性评估科学主张，而这些能力的交互作用难以通过单独任务评估来衡量。

Method: PAPERMIND从七个科学领域的真实论文构建，包含四个互补任务族：(1)多模态定位——将文本与视觉信息关联；(2)实验解释——理解和评估实验证据；(3)跨源证据推理——综合多来源信息；(4)批判性评估——评估科学主张的有效性。通过多任务联合分析实现诊断性评估。

Result: 对开源和闭源多模态LLM的广泛实验表明，各任务均存在一致的性能差距，揭示了当前模型在综合科学推理和批判性评估方面面临的持续挑战。

Conclusion: PAPERMIND能够诊断性评估综合科学推理行为，这些行为难以通过孤立任务评估来衡量。基准测试为推动科学AI系统的能力提升提供了标准化评估框架和数据集。

Abstract: Understanding scientific papers requires more than answering isolated questions or summarizing content. It involves an integrated reasoning process that grounds textual and visual information, interprets experimental evidence, synthesizes information across sources, and critically evaluates scientific claims. However, existing benchmarks typically assess these abilities in isolation, making it difficult to evaluate scientific paper understanding as a unified set of interacting cognitive abilities. In this work, we introduce PAPERMIND, a benchmark designed to evaluate integrated and agent-oriented scientific reasoning over research papers. PAPERMIND is constructed from real scientific papers across seven domains, including agriculture, biology, chemistry, computer science, medicine, physics, and economics. It comprises four complementary task families that collectively operationalize distinct cognitive facets of scientific paper reasoning, including multimodal grounding, experimental interpretation, cross-source evidence reasoning, and critical assessment. By analyzing model behavior across multiple tasks, PAPERMIND enables a diagnostic evaluation of integrated scientific reasoning behaviors that are difficult to assess through isolated task evaluations.
