Extensive experiments on both opensource and closed-source multimodal LLMs reveal consistent performance gaps across tasks, highlighting persistent challenges in integrated scientific reasoning and critique. Our benchmark and dataset are available at https:// github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/PaperMind.

</details>


### [214] [From Tokens to Concepts: Leveraging SAE for SPLADE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21511)
*Yuxuan Zong,Mathias Vast,Basile Van Cooten,Laure Soulier,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出用稀疏自编码器(SAE)学习语义概念潜在空间来替代SPLADE模型中的底层词汇表，解决词汇依赖带来的多义词、同义词问题及多语言/多模态挑战，提出SAE-SPLADE模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPLADE等学习型稀疏检索模型依赖底层词汇表，存在多义词和同义词问题，且难以支持多语言和多模态场景。

Method: 用SAE学习的语义概念潜在空间替换SPLADE的底层词汇表，探索兼容性、训练方法，分析SAE-SPLADE与传统SPLADE的差异。

Result: SAE-SPLADE在域内和域外任务上达到与SPLADE相当的检索性能，同时效率有所提升。

Conclusion: SAE-SPLADE可作为SPLADE的有效替代方案，在保持性能的同时提供更好的效率。

Abstract: Learned Sparse IR models, such as SPLADE, offer an excellent efficiency-effectiveness tradeoff. However, they rely on the underlying backbone vocabulary, which might hinder performance (polysemicity and synonymy) and pose a challenge for multi-lingual and multi-modal usages. To solve this limitation, we propose to replace the backbone vocabulary with a latent space of semantic concepts learned using Sparse Auto-Encoders (SAE). Throughout this paper, we study the compatibility of these 2 concepts, explore training approaches, and analyze the differences between our SAE-SPLADE model and traditional SPLADE models. Our experiments demonstrate that SAE-SPLADE achieves retrieval performance comparable to SPLADE on both in-domain and out-of-domain tasks while offering improved efficiency.

</details>


### [215] [Pre-trained LLMs Meet Sequential Recommenders: Efficient User-Centric Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21536)
*Nikita Severin,Danil Kartushov,Vladislav Urzhumov,Vladislav Kulikov,Oksana Konovalova,Alexey Grishanov,Anton Klenitskiy,Artem Fatkulin,Alexey Vasilev,Andrey Savchenko,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识蒸馏方法，利用预训练LLM生成的文本用户画像增强序列推荐系统，同时在服务时无需LLM推理，从而兼顾语义理解能力与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐系统仅能捕捉用户交互模式，难以建模丰富的用户语义信息；LLM虽具备推理能力可增强用户理解，但直接将LLM集成到推荐系统会在实时服务时产生高昂的推理成本。

Method: 提出一种知识蒸馏框架，将预训练LLM生成的文本用户画像知识迁移到序列推荐模型。该方法无需修改模型架构、无需微调LLM，仅在训练阶段利用LLM生成的画像进行知识转移，服务阶段保持传统序列模型的推理流程。

Result: 方法在保持传统序列模型推理效率的同时，有效融合了LLM的语义理解能力，实现了效率与性能的平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为将大语言模型能力融入推荐系统提供了一条高效可行的技术路径，无需在服务时引入LLM推理开销，即可利用其语义知识提升推荐效果。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems have achieved significant success in modeling temporal user behavior but remain limited in capturing rich user semantics beyond interaction patterns. Large Language Models (LLMs) present opportunities to enhance user understanding with their reasoning capabilities, yet existing integration approaches create prohibitive inference costs in real time. To address these limitations, we present a novel knowledge distillation method that utilizes textual user profile generated by pre-trained LLMs into sequential recommenders without requiring LLM inference at serving time. The resulting approach maintains the inference efficiency of traditional sequential models while requiring neither architectural modifications nor LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [216] [Counterfactual Multi-task Learning for Delayed Conversion Modeling in E-commerce Sales Pre-Promotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21675)
*Xin Song,Kaiyuan Li,Jinxin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CM-DCM模型，针对促销前用户行为延迟转化特征解决CVR预测难题，通过多任务架构、用户行为门控和反事实因果方法提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVR预测研究主要关注促销当日的转化预测，忽视了促销前这一关键阶段。促销前用户行为模式独特——用户浏览加购但延迟到促销日才购买，导致促销前CVR显著偏低。传统延迟反馈建模方法未考虑促销场景下转化时间分布的特殊性（集中于促销当日），难以准确建模从加购到延迟转化的转移概率。

Method: 提出CM-DCM模型，包含三个核心创新模块：(1) 多任务架构：联合建模直接转化与延迟转化，共享历史促销前数据；(2) 个性化用户行为门控模块：缓解促销前期数据稀疏问题；(3) 反事实因果方法：建模加购行为到延迟转化的转移概率。

Result: 离线实验表明CM-DCM在促销前场景下优于基线模型；在线A/B测试验证了广告收入、延迟转化GMV和整体GMV的显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过融合多任务学习与因果推断，CM-DCM有效解决了促销前CVR预测的独特挑战，为电商促销场景提供了切实有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Sales promotions, as short-term incentives to stimulate product purchases, play a pivotal role in modern e-commerce marketing strategies. During promotional events, user behavior patterns exhibit distinct characteristics compared to regular periods. In the pre-promotion phase, users typically engage in product search and browsing without immediate purchases, adding items to carts in anticipation of promotional discounts. This behavior leads to delayed conversions, resulting in significantly lower conversion rates (CVR) before the promotion day. Although existing research has made progress in CVR prediction for promotion days using historical data, it largely overlooks the critical pre-promotion period. And delayed feedback modeling has been extensively studied, current approaches fail to account for the unique distribution shifts in conversion behavior before promotional events, where delayed conversions predominantly occur on the promotion day rather than over continuous time windows. To address these limitations, we propose the Counterfactual Multi-task Delayed Conversion Model (CM-DCM), which leverages historical pre-promotion data to enhance CVR prediction for both delayed and direct conversions. Our model incorporates three key innovations: (i) A multi-task architecture that jointly models direct and delayed conversions using historical pre-promotion data; (ii) A personalized user behavior gating module to mitigate data sparsity issues during brief pre-promotion periods; (iii) A counterfactual causal approach to model the transition probability from add-to-cart (ATC) to delayed conversion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CM-DCM outperforms baselines in pre-promotion scenarios. Online A/B tests during major promotional events showed significant improvements in advertising revenue, delayed conversion GMV, and overall GMV, validating the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [217] [Multistakeholder Impacts of Profile Portability in a Recommender Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21750)
*Anas Buhayh,Elizabeth McKinnie,Clement Canel,Robin Burke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨推荐系统生态中的算法多元主义（algorithmic pluralism，即"中间件"方案），即解耦推荐算法与平台，允许用户选择偏好的算法。研究重点关注数据可携带性政策对用户模型和利益相关者的影响，发现不同数据可携带性场景对各推荐算法的用户效用产生不同影响，并提出公平推荐生态系统设计的政策建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统优化主要关注算法层面的干预（如多目标模型或重排序），而对推荐生态系统的结构性变革研究不足。关键问题是：当用户切换算法时，用户数据会发生什么变化？数据可携带性政策如何影响用户模型和利益相关者的结果？

Method: 基于仿真的研究方法，分析算法多元主义场景（即算法与平台解耦、用户可选择算法），并评估不同数据可携带性政策对推荐系统中用户效用和各利益相关者结果的影响。

Result: 数据可携带性场景对不同推荐算法的用户效用产生差异化影响；算法选择机制有利于小众消费者和内容提供者；不同数据可携带性政策对生态系统各方的效果各异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了数据可携带性政策的关键设计考量，为构建公平的推荐生态系统提供了政策启示；强调了算法解耦与用户选择权在推荐系统治理中的重要性。

Abstract: Optimizing outcomes for multiple stakeholders in recommender systems has historically focused on algorithmic interventions, such as developing multi-objective models or re-ranking results from existing algorithms. However, structural changes to the recommendation ecosystem itself remain understudied. This paper explores the implications of algorithmic pluralism (also known as "middleware" in the governance literature), in which recommendation algorithms are decoupled from platforms, enabling users to select their preferred algorithm. Prior simulation work demonstrates that algorithmic choice benefits niche consumers and providers. Yet this approach raises critical questions about user modeling in the context of data portability: when users switch algorithms, what happens to their data? Noting that multiple data portability regulations have emerged to strengthen user data ownership and control. We examine how such policies affect user models and stakeholders' outcomes in recommendation setting. Our findings reveal that data portability scenarios produce varying effects on user utility across different recommendation algorithms. We highlight key policy considerations and implications for designing equitable recommendation ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [SQLyzr: A Comprehensive Benchmark and Evaluation Platform for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21214)
*Sepideh Abedini,M. Tamer Özsu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SQLyzr是一个全面的Text-to-SQL模型基准测试和评估平台，解决了现有基准测试的局限性（如单一聚合分数、缺乏真实场景评估、对不同查询类型的行为洞察有限），提供多样化评估指标、真实工作负载对齐、细粒度查询分类、错误分析和交互式图形界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL评估基准存在三个主要问题：(1) 仅依赖单一聚合分数；(2) 缺乏真实场景下的评估设置；(3) 对模型在不同查询类型下的行为洞察有限。这些局限性阻碍了对Text-to-SQL模型的有效诊断和迭代改进。

Method: SQLyzr通过以下方式实现全面评估：(1) 整合多样化评估指标以捕捉生成SQL的多个维度；(2) 通过真实世界SQL使用模式的工作负载对齐和数据库扩展实现更真实的评估；(3) 支持细粒度查询分类和错误分析；(4) 支持工作负载增强；(5) 提供交互式图形界面，允许用户自定义评估设置和分析细粒度报告。

Result: 演示了SQLyzr的核心功能：用户可通过图形界面自定义评估设置、分析细粒度报告、探索平台附加功能。项目代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: SQLyzr通过解决现有基准测试的关键局限性，促进Text-to-SQL模型的评估和迭代改进。其开源特性和交互式平台有望推动该领域的研究进步。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL models have significantly improved with the adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs), leading to their increasing use in real-world applications. Although many benchmarks exist for evaluating the performance of text-to-SQL models, they often rely on a single aggregate score, lack evaluation under realistic settings, and provide limited insight into model behaviour across different query types. In this work, we present SQLyzr, a comprehensive benchmark and evaluation platform for text-to-SQL models. SQLyzr incorporates a diverse set of evaluation metrics that capture multiple aspects of generated queries, while enabling more realistic evaluation through workload alignment with real-world SQL usage patterns and database scaling. It further supports fine-grained query classification, error analysis, and workload augmentation, allowing users to better diagnose and improve text-to-SQL models. This demonstration showcases these capabilities through an interactive experience. Through SQLyzr's graphical interface, users can customize evaluation settings, analyze fine-grained reports, and explore additional features of the platform. We envision that SQLyzr facilitates the evaluation and iterative improvement of text-to-SQL models by addressing key limitations of existing benchmarks. The source code of SQLyzr is available at https://github.com/sepideh-abedini/SQLyzr.

</details>


### [219] [An Alternate Agentic AI Architecture (It's About the Data)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21413)
*Fabian Wenz,Felix Treutwein,Kai Arenja,Çagatay Demiralp,Michael Stonebraker*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文批评了当前将大语言模型作为企业AI主导方法的思路，指出企业面临的核心问题是数据集成而非推理能力不足。作者提出RUBICON架构，采用类似SQL的显式查询语言AQL和源特定包装器来连接异构企业数据源，实现可追溯、可审计、可预测的智能查询，而非依赖黑盒的LLM调用链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的"智能体AI"方法让大语言模型动态选择工具、发出子查询并综合结果。作者认为这种方法对企业来说是误导性的：企业面临的不是推理缺陷问题，而是数据集成问题。企业关键信息分散在异构系统（数据库、文档、外部服务等）中，每个系统都有各自的查询语言、模式、访问控制和性能约束。当前的LLM架构针对非结构化文本推理优化，将企业系统视为语料库或由黑盒组件调用的外部工具，导致模式丰富、治理完善、性能关键的数据系统与以文本为中心、概率性的LLM架构之间产生不匹配，引发透明度有限、正确性保证薄弱、性能不可预测等问题。

Method: 提出RUBICON架构，采用数据管理原则，使用AQL（Agentic Query Language）——一个由Find、From、Where组成的小型显式查询代数，通过源特定包装器执行以强制访问控制、模式对齐和结果规范化。所有中间结果都是可见和可检查的，复杂问题被分解为结构化的、可审计的查询计划，而非隐藏的LLM调用链。

Result: 通过引入显式查询结构、基于包装器的中介和基于成本的优化，实现了智能搜索的广度，同时保留了企业在环境中所需的可追溯性、确定性和信任。

Conclusion: Enterprise AI应该被视为系统问题而非提示工程问题。通过重新引入显式查询结构、基于包装器的中介机制和基于成本的优化，可以在保持企业环境所需的可追溯性、确定性和信任度的同时，获得智能搜索的广度。

Abstract: For the last several years, the dominant narrative in "agentic AI" has been that large language models should orchestrate information access by dynamically selecting tools, issuing sub-queries, and synthesizing results. We argue this approach is misguided: enterprises do not suffer from a reasoning deficit, but from a data integration problem.
  Enterprises are data-centric: critical information is scattered across heterogeneous systems (e.g., databases, documents, and external services), each with its own query language, schema, access controls, and performance constraints. In contrast, contemporary LLM-based architectures are optimized for reasoning over unstructured text and treat enterprise systems as either corpora or external tools invoked by a black-box component. This creates a mismatch between schema-rich, governed, performance-critical data systems and text-centric, probabilistic LLM architectures, leading to limited transparency, weak correctness guarantees, and unpredictable performance.
  In this paper, we present RUBICON, an alternative architecture grounded in data management principles. Instead of delegating orchestration to an opaque agent, we introduce AQL (Agentic Query Language), a small, explicit query algebra - Find, From, and Where - executed through source-specific wrappers that enforce access control, schema alignment, and result normalization. All intermediate results are visible and inspectable. Complex questions are decomposed into structured, auditable query plans rather than hidden chains of LLM calls.
  Our thesis is simple: enterprise AI is not a prompt engineering problem; it is a systems problem. By reintroducing explicit query structure, wrapper-based mediation, and cost-based optimization, we obtain the breadth of agentic search while preserving traceability, determinism, and trust in enterprise environments.
